9

This can also be verified through explicit calculation by splitting the quantities in (
3.22
) into their chiral contributions that have fixed transformation properties. Due to the ‘hidden’ chiral projectors, all the contributions that do not transform appropriately are projected out of the trace.

In total, we have shown that the coupling flow operator obtained from dimensional reduction on the Landau gauge hypersurface is

\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\leftarrow}}{{R_{g}}}[\mathscr{A}]\ =\ {\textstyle\frac{1}{32}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\longleftarrow}}{{{\textstyle\frac{\delta}{\delta\mathscr{A}_{\Gamma}}}}}{P}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94446pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94446pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.0625pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1875pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\Sigma}}}_{{{\Gamma}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.70836pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.70836pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.5389pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.52777pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\ \mathrm{tr}\Bigl\{(\mathscr{C}_{\Sigma})^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}S^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{C}}}\not{\mathscr{A}}{{}^{{{C}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{D}}}}\times\not{\mathscr{A}}^{*}{{}^{{{D}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{A}}}}\Bigl\}\ .

(3.23)

3.3
Unified R-symmetric framework

The goal of this subsection is to reconcile the two results (
3.7
) and (
3.23
), since they are clearly not identical. For simplicity, we for now restrict to the Landau gauge, for which the two results read

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\leftarrow}}{{R_{g}}}[\mathscr{A}]\ =\ {\textstyle\frac{1}{8}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\longleftarrow}}{{{\textstyle\frac{\delta}{\delta\mathscr{A}_{\Gamma}}}}}{P}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94446pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94446pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.0625pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1875pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\Sigma}}}_{{{\Gamma}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.70836pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.70836pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.5389pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.52777pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\ \mathrm{tr}\Bigl\{(\mathscr{C}_{\Sigma})^{{{4}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}S^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{C}}}\not{\mathscr{A}}{{}^{{{C}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{D}}}}\times\not{\mathscr{A}}^{*}{{}^{{{D}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{4}}}}\Bigl\}

\displaystyle\text{from canonical construction}\ ,

(3.24)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\leftarrow}}{{R_{g}}}[\mathscr{A}]\ =\ {\textstyle\frac{1}{32}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\longleftarrow}}{{{\textstyle\frac{\delta}{\delta\mathscr{A}_{\Gamma}}}}}{P}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94446pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94446pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.0625pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1875pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\Sigma}}}_{{{\Gamma}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.70836pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.70836pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.5389pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.52777pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\ \mathrm{tr}\Bigl\{(\mathscr{C}_{\Sigma})^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}S^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{C}}}\not{\mathscr{A}}{{}^{{{C}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{D}}}}\times\not{\mathscr{A}}^{*}{{}^{{{D}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{A}}}}\Bigl\}

\displaystyle\text{from dimensional reduction}\ .

(3.25)

We begin this discussion with two universal observations. Firstly, the general definition of the coupling flow operator
R_{g}[\phi]
(
1.5
) shows that it maps real observables to other real observables.
10
10

10

That is, modulo imaginary terms that vanish when taking the expectation value. For simplicity, we ignore the possibility of such extra imaginary terms.
Hence, we require the kernel
K
of the coupling flow operator

R_{g}[\phi]\ =\ \int\!\mathrm{d}x\ K[\phi;\;x]\,\frac{\delta}{\delta\phi(x)}

(3.26)

to be real. Note that for our cases and notation above, the kernel carries a ten-dimensional index
\Gamma
, so that

\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\leftarrow}}{{R_{g}}}[\mathscr{A}]\ =\ \stackrel{{\scriptstyle\longleftarrow}}{{{\textstyle\frac{\delta}{\delta\mathscr{A}_{\Gamma}}}}}K_{\Gamma}\ ,

(3.27)

with implicit integration. Secondly, we recall the three conditions (
1.10
), (
1.11
) for a coupling flow operator in a general gauge theory

(\partial_{g}+R_{g}[\phi])S^{\mathrm{b}}_{g}[\phi]\ =\ 0\ ,\qquad(\partial_{g}+R_{g}[\phi])S^{\mathrm{f}}_{g}[\phi]\ =\ \int\!\mathrm{d}x\ \frac{\delta K[\phi;\;x]}{\delta\phi(x)}\ ,\qquad(\partial_{g}+R_{g}[\phi])\mathcal{G}(\phi)\ =\ 0\ .

(3.28)

It is easy to see that given two coupling flow operators
R_{g}^{(1)}
and
R_{g}^{(2)}
, the linear combination

R^{\prime}_{g}\ :=\ pR_{g}^{(1)}+qR_{g}^{(2)}\quad\text{with}\quad p,q\ \in\ \mathbb{R}\quad\text{and}\quad p+q\ =\ 1

(3.29)

is again a coupling flow operator (i.e. satisfies (
3.28
)). Thus, the coupling flow operator obeys a principle of superposition with real coefficients that add up to one.

The next ingredient is the fact that in the canonical construction we had to single out one of the supersymmetries to obtain a working superfield formalism. In this work, we chose the ‘fourth’ one, resulting in the index 4 at the beginning and the end of the trace in (
3.24
). This choice is of course arbitrary. We could have equivalently chosen any of the three other supersymmetries. This, together with the principle of superposition allows us (in the Landau gauge) to build a more general coupling flow operator by inserting a diagonal matrix with trace four (or as we prefer, the identity plus a traceless matrix
\mathrm{L}
) in R-space in the trace in the kernel. In anticipation of the rest of this discussion, we write down the general ansatz

\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\leftarrow}}{{R_{g}}}[\mathscr{A}]\ =\ {\textstyle\frac{1}{32}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\longleftarrow}}{{{\textstyle\frac{\delta}{\delta\mathscr{A}_{\Gamma}}}}}{P}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94446pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94446pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.0625pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1875pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\Sigma}}}_{{{\Gamma}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.70836pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.70836pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.5389pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.52777pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\ \mathrm{tr}\Bigl\{(\mathscr{C}_{\Sigma})^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}S^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{C}}}\not{\mathscr{A}}{{}^{{{C}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{D}}}}\times\not{\mathscr{A}}^{*}{{}^{{{D}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{E}}}}(\delta^{{{E}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{A}}}+\mathrm{L}^{{{E}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{A}}})\Bigl\}\ .

(3.30)

The two cases (
3.24
), (
3.25
) correspond to

\mathrm{L}\ =\ \operatorname{diag}(-1,-1,-1,+3)\quad\text{and}\quad\mathrm{L}\ =\ 0\ ,

(3.31)

respectively. From the discussion so far we know that we can reach any element of

\Bigl\{\mathrm{L}\ =\ \operatorname{diag}(q_{1},q_{2},q_{3},q_{4})\quad\text{with}\quad\sum_{i}q_{i}\ =\ 0\ \Bigr\}\ =:\ \mathfrak{h}\ ,

(3.32)

the Cartan subalgebra of the Lie algebra
\mathfrak{su}(4)
.
By applying an
\mathrm{SU}(4)
R transformation on the various factors in (
3.30
), we can deduce how
\mathrm{L}
effectively transforms under R transformations. One finds that the underlying structure of the chiral projectors requires that just like the Majorana spinors (
2.3
),
\mathrm{L}
splits up into two chiral parts

\mathrm{L}\ =\ L\ \mathrm{P}^{-}+L^{*}\ \mathrm{P}^{+}

(3.33)

that are complex conjugate to each other. The matrix
L
transforms in the adjoint
\mathbf{15}
of
\mathrm{SU}(4)

L\quad\longrightarrow\quad ULU^{\dagger}\ ,\quad\quad\textrm{with}\quad U\ \in\ \mathrm{SU}(4)\ .

(3.34)

We notice that (
3.34
) preserves zero trace and hermiticity
L^{\dagger}=L
so that the group action of
\mathrm{SU}(4)
on
\mathfrak{h}
(
3.32
) generates the entire Lie algebra
\mathfrak{su}(4)
. Any
L\ \in\ \mathfrak{su}(4)
can be unitarily diagonalized such that we can classify it in terms of its four eigenvalues
q_{i}
. Invariants under the adjoint action are
\mathrm{tr}\ L^{m}
for any integer
m\geq 1
, but with only the first four

\mathrm{tr}\ L\ =\ \sum q_{i}\ =\ 0\ ,\quad\mathrm{tr}\ L^{2}\ =\ \sum q_{i}^{2}\ ,\quad\mathrm{tr}\ L^{3}\ =\ \sum q_{i}^{3}\ ,\quad\mathrm{tr}\ L^{4}\ =\ \sum q_{i}^{4}

(3.35)

functionally independent. We can equivalently characterize a generic orbit by the eigenvalues
(q_{1},\ q_{2},\ q_{3},\ q_{4})
with
\sum\ q_{i}=0
or by
(\mathrm{tr}\ L^{2},\ \mathrm{tr}\ L^{3},\ \mathrm{tr}\ L^{4})
. This gives us three real parameters matching the dimension of the Cartan subalgebra. For a generic
L\ \in\ \mathfrak{su}(4)
with all eigenvalues
q_{i}
distinct, the stabilizer of the adjoint action is the maximal torus
\mathrm{S}(\mathrm{U}(1)^{4})\ \cong\ \mathrm{U}(1)^{3}
and its orbit under the action is the 12-dimensional flag manifold

{\mathchoice{\raisebox{3.75pt}{$\displaystyle{\mathrm{SU}(4)}$}\mkern-5.0mu\diagup\mkern-4.0mu\raisebox{-4.32pt}{$\displaystyle{\mathrm{U}(1)^{3}}$}}{\raisebox{3.75pt}{$\textstyle{\mathrm{SU}(4)}$}\mkern-5.0mu\diagup\mkern-4.0mu\raisebox{-4.07pt}{$\textstyle{\mathrm{U}(1)^{3}}$}}{\raisebox{2.625pt}{$\scriptstyle{\mathrm{SU}(4)}$}\mkern-5.0mu\diagup\mkern-4.0mu\raisebox{-3.12001pt}{$\scriptstyle{\mathrm{U}(1)^{3}}$}}{\raisebox{1.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathrm{SU}(4)}$}\mkern-5.0mu\diagup\mkern-4.0mu\raisebox{-2.33333pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathrm{U}(1)^{3}}$}}}\ .

(3.36)

For singular
L
, i.e. some eigenvalues
q_{i}
coinciding, the stabilizer is larger and the orbit smaller. Table
2
summarizes all cases.

of

q_{i}
’s to the dimension of the orbit

The fully degenerate case corresponds to
L=0
(
3.25
). It is a fixed point under all
\mathrm{SU}(4)
transformations. The canonical construction on the other hand led to a configuration where three of the
q_{i}
’s were equal, so that only an
\mathrm{S}(\operatorname{U}(3)\times\operatorname{U}(1))
subgroup leaves the configuration invariant. This suggests that for
L
with three degenerate
q_{i}
’s, the points in its orbit under the adjoint action of
\mathrm{SU}(4)
are those that originate from an off-shell formalism and hence allow for an arbitrary choice of the gauge fixing function. For these cases, when choosing gauges other than the Landau gauge, the second term in (
3.7
) has to be added to the general formula (
3.30
).

In Appendix
C
, we directly check that (
3.30
) indeed satisfies the infinitesimal conditions of a coupling flow operator.

4
Nicolai maps

In this section we use the notation of
[
13
]
to write down Nicolai maps to second order. We briefly recall the most important aspects of this compact notation. Derivatives of the scalar propagator
C
are simply written as indices, so that e.g.
\partial_{\mu}\partial_{\nu}C\equiv C_{\mu\nu}
. Further, all objects are implicitly matrices in color space with the exception that the last quantity in each term is a vector in color space. Moreover, implicit integration kernels are convoluted with insertions of bosonic fields
A
or
\varphi
.

A Nicolai map for
\mathcal{N}=\,4
SYM to
\mathcal{O}(g^{2})
in the Landau gauge was already found in
[
9
]
by directly reducing the result from
\mathcal{N}=\,1

D=10

T_{g}\mathscr{A}_{\Sigma}\ =\ \mathscr{A}_{\Sigma}-gC^{\Theta}\mathscr{A}_{\Sigma}\mathscr{A}_{\Theta}+{\textstyle\frac{3}{2}}g^{2}C^{\Theta}\mathscr{A}^{\Gamma}C_{[\Sigma}\mathscr{A}_{\Theta}\mathscr{A}_{\Gamma]}+\mathcal{O}(g^{3})\ ,

(4.1)

down to four dimensions with the simple prescription
\mathscr{A}\ =\ (A_{\mu},\ \varphi_{i})
and
\partial_{3+i}\equiv 0
, which leads to

\displaystyle T_{g}A_{\mu}\ =\ A_{\mu}-gC^{\rho}A_{\mu}A_{\rho}+{\textstyle\frac{3}{2}}g^{2}C^{\rho}A^{\lambda}C_{[\mu}A_{\rho}A_{\lambda]}+g^{2}C^{\rho}\varphi_{i}C_{[\mu}A_{\rho]}\varphi_{i}+\mathcal{O}(g^{3})\ ,

(4.2)

\displaystyle T_{g}\varphi_{i}\ =\ \varphi_{i}-gC^{\rho}\varphi_{i}A_{\rho}+g^{2}C^{[\rho}A^{\lambda]}C_{\lambda}\varphi_{i}A_{\rho}+{\textstyle\frac{1}{2}}g^{2}C^{\rho}\varphi_{j}C_{\rho}\varphi_{j}\varphi_{i}+\mathcal{O}(g^{3})\ .

(4.3)

In this section, we want to explicitly show the ambiguity of the
\mathcal{N}=\,4
map by computing (Appendix
D
) and testing (Appendix
E
) four different maps to second order in the Landau gauge corresponding to the points

L\ =\ \operatorname{diag}(+3,-1,-1,-1)\ ,\ ...\ ,\ \operatorname{diag}(-1,-1,-1,+3)\ ,

(4.4)

in
\mathfrak{su}(4)
and denote the respective coupling flow operators as

\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\leftarrow}}{{R_{g}}}{{}^{(A)}}[\mathscr{A}]\ =\ {\textstyle\frac{1}{8}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\longleftarrow}}{{{\textstyle\frac{\delta}{\delta\mathscr{A}_{\Gamma}}}}}{P}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94446pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94446pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.0625pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1875pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\Sigma}}}_{{{\Gamma}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.70836pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.70836pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.5389pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.52777pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\ \mathrm{tr}\Bigl\{(\mathscr{C}_{\Sigma})^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}S^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{C}}}\not{\mathscr{A}}{{}^{{{C}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{D}}}}\times\not{\mathscr{A}}^{*}{{}^{{{D}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{A}}}}\Bigl\}\ .

(4.5)

with no sum over
A=1,2,3,4
. In general
[
12
]
, the Nicolai map can be obtained from the perturbative expansion

R_{g}[\mathscr{A}]\ =\ \sum_{k=1}^{\infty}g^{k-1}\mathrm{R}_{k}[\mathscr{A}]\ =\ \mathrm{R}_{1}[\mathscr{A}]+g\,\mathrm{R}_{2}[\mathscr{A}]+g^{2}\mathrm{R}_{3}[\mathscr{A}]+\ldots\ ,

(4.6)

via

\displaystyle T_{g}\mathscr{A}

\displaystyle=\ \mathscr{A}\ -\ g\,\mathrm{R}_{1}\mathscr{A}\ -\ {\textstyle\frac{1}{2}}g^{2}\bigl(\mathrm{R}_{2}-\mathrm{R}_{1}^{2}\bigr)\mathscr{A}\ +\ {\cal O}(g^{3})\ .

(4.7)

When putting all the contributions together, we find the four distinct Nicolai maps

\displaystyle T_{g}^{(4)}A_{\mu}\ =

\displaystyle A_{\mu}-gC^{\rho}A_{\mu}A_{\rho}+{\textstyle\frac{3}{2}}g^{2}C^{\rho}A^{\lambda}C_{[\mu}A_{\rho}A_{\lambda]}+g^{2}C^{\rho}\varphi_{i}C_{[\mu}A_{\rho]}\varphi_{i}

(4.8)

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{\displaystyle-{\textstyle\frac{1}{2}}g^{2}\Pi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\epsilon_{\nu\lambda\rho\sigma}\sum_{J=1}^{3}[C^{\lambda}\varphi_{J}C^{\rho}\varphi_{J+3}A^{\sigma}-C^{\lambda}\varphi_{J+3}C^{\rho}\varphi_{J}A^{\sigma}+C^{\lambda}A^{\rho}C^{\sigma}\varphi_{J+3}\varphi_{J}]}+\mathcal{O}(g^{3})\ ,

\displaystyle T_{g}^{(K)}A_{\mu}\ =

\displaystyle A_{\mu}-gC^{\rho}A_{\mu}A_{\rho}+{\textstyle\frac{3}{2}}g^{2}C^{\rho}A^{\lambda}C_{[\mu}A_{\rho}A_{\lambda]}+g^{2}C^{\rho}\varphi_{i}C_{[\mu}A_{\rho]}\varphi_{i}

(4.9)

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{\displaystyle+{\textstyle\frac{1}{2}}g^{2}\Pi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\epsilon_{\nu\lambda\rho\sigma}\sum_{J=1}^{3}(-)^{\delta_{KJ}}[C^{\lambda}\varphi_{J}C^{\rho}\varphi_{J+3}A^{\sigma}-C^{\lambda}\varphi_{J+3}C^{\rho}\varphi_{J}A^{\sigma}+C^{\lambda}A^{\rho}C^{\sigma}\varphi_{J+3}\varphi_{J}]}+\mathcal{O}(g^{3})\ ,

and

\displaystyle T_{g}^{(4)}\varphi_{I}\ =

\displaystyle\varphi_{I}-gC^{\rho}\varphi_{I}A_{\rho}+g^{2}C^{[\rho}A^{\lambda]}C_{\lambda}\varphi_{I}A_{\rho}+{\textstyle\frac{1}{2}}g^{2}C^{\rho}\varphi_{j}C_{\rho}\varphi_{j}\varphi_{I}

(4.10)

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{\displaystyle-{\textstyle\frac{1}{4}}g^{2}\epsilon_{\mu\nu\rho\lambda}[C^{\mu}\varphi_{I+3}C^{\nu}A^{\rho}A^{\lambda}+2C^{\mu}A^{\nu}C^{\rho}\varphi_{I+3}A^{\lambda}]}

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{\displaystyle-{\textstyle\frac{1}{2}}g^{2}C^{\rho}\sum_{J=1}^{3}[\varphi_{I+3}C_{\rho}\varphi_{J+3}\varphi_{J}+\varphi_{J}C_{\rho}\varphi_{I+3}\varphi_{J+3}-\varphi_{J+3}C_{\rho}\varphi_{I+3}\varphi_{J}]}+\mathcal{O}(g^{3})\ ,

\displaystyle T_{g}^{(K)}\varphi_{I}\ =

\displaystyle\varphi_{I}-gC^{\rho}\varphi_{I}A_{\rho}+g^{2}C^{[\rho}A^{\lambda]}C_{\lambda}\varphi_{I}A_{\rho}+{\textstyle\frac{1}{2}}g^{2}C^{\rho}\varphi_{j}C_{\rho}\varphi_{j}\varphi_{I}

(4.11)

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{\displaystyle+{\textstyle\frac{1}{4}}g^{2}\epsilon_{\mu\nu\rho\lambda}(-)^{\delta_{IK}}[C^{\mu}\varphi_{I+3}C^{\nu}A^{\rho}A^{\lambda}+2C^{\mu}A^{\nu}C^{\rho}\varphi_{I+3}A^{\lambda}]}

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{\displaystyle-{\textstyle\frac{1}{2}}g^{2}C^{\rho}(-)^{\delta_{IK}}\sum_{J=1}^{3}[\varphi_{I+3}C_{\rho}\varphi_{J+3}\varphi_{J}+\varphi_{J}C_{\rho}\varphi_{I+3}\varphi_{J+3}-\varphi_{J+3}C_{\rho}\varphi_{I+3}\varphi_{J}]}

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{\displaystyle+g^{2}C^{\rho}[\varphi_{I+3}C_{\rho}\varphi_{K+3}\varphi_{K}+\varphi_{K}C_{\rho}\varphi_{I+3}\varphi_{K+3}-\varphi_{K+3}C_{\rho}\varphi_{I+3}\varphi_{K}]}+\mathcal{O}(g^{3})\ ,

and the blue parts of
T_{g}^{(4)}\varphi_{I+3}
,
T_{g}^{(K)}\varphi_{I+3}
are given by those of (
4.10
), (
4.11
) with
I
and
I{+}3
exchanged and an overall minus sign on the r.h.s., while the black terms are obtained by simply replacing
I
with
I{+}3
. Note that the black parts of all four maps exactly equal each other and the result from dimensional reduction (
4.2
), (
4.3
), whereas the blue parts differ in signs for the four choices
A=1,2,3,4
. By investigating the explicit contributions (Appendix
D
) to the coupling flow operators
R_{g}^{(A)}
, it is easy to check that the symmetric superposition

R_{g}\ :=\ {\textstyle\frac{1}{4}}(R_{g}^{(1)}+R_{g}^{(2)}+R_{g}^{(3)}+R_{g}^{(4)})\ ,

(4.12)

exactly yields the result from dimensional reduction, as expected. Note that while we can superimpose coupling flow operators, this is not the case for the Nicolai map (
1.8
), since it is not linear in
R_{g}
. In Appendix
E
, we show through explicit computations that all four maps that we have found in this section indeed satisfy the necessary conditions for a Nicolai map to second order.

5
Conclusions and outlook

In this work we have initiated a systematic study of the Nicolai map in
{\cal N}=\,4
supersymmetric Yang–Mills theory in four dimensions. Our main result is the explicit form of the coupling flow operator in the Landau gauge

\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\leftarrow}}{{R_{g}}}[\mathscr{A}]\ =\ {\textstyle\frac{1}{32}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\longleftarrow}}{{{\textstyle\frac{\delta}{\delta\mathscr{A}_{\Gamma}}}}}{P}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94446pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94446pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.0625pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1875pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\Sigma}}}_{{{\Gamma}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.70836pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.70836pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.5389pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.52777pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\ \mathrm{tr}\Bigl\{(\mathscr{C}_{\Sigma})^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}S^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{C}}}\not{\mathscr{A}}{{}^{{{C}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{D}}}}\times\not{\mathscr{A}}^{*}{{}^{{{D}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{E}}}}(\delta^{{{E}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{A}}}+\mathrm{L}^{{{E}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{A}}})\Bigl\}\ ,

with

\mathrm{L}\ =\ L\ \mathrm{P}^{-}+L^{*}\ \mathrm{P}^{+}\ ,

and
L
any element in the Lie algebra
\mathfrak{su}(4)
. Most importantly, it follows that the operator is subject to a 15-dimensional ambiguity. This can be traced back to the theory-intrinsic
\mathrm{SU}(4)
R-symmetry. However, by construction, correlators do not depend on the choice of
L
. Building up on previous results in
{\cal N}=\,1
SYM and with the help of very compact notation, the knowledge of
R_{g}
allows for an analogous perturbative construction of the Nicolai map of
{\cal N}=\,4
SYM via the universal formula
T_{g}=\overrightarrow{\cal P}\exp\bigl\{-\int_{0}^{g}\mathrm{d}h\,R_{h}[\mathscr{A}]\bigr\}
.

These first steps suggest many future analyses. Natural open questions relate to the effect of including a topological term in the theory, a better understanding of general gauges and a graphical representation for the perturbative expansion. As a potential application of this work, one could compute explicit
{\cal N}=\,4
quantum correlators
\bigl\langle X[\mathscr{A}]\bigr\rangle_{g}=\bigl\langle X[T_{g}^{-1}\mathscr{A}]\bigr\rangle_{0}
with the inverse Nicolai map. Critical future investigations concern how exactly the ambiguity in the coupling flow operator translates to the Nicolai map and, more distantly related to that, whether the framework of the Nicolai map might hint at an integrable structure of
{\cal N}=\,4
SYM.

Details on the
\mathcal{N}=\,1
superfield formalism

In the conventions from
[
17

11

up to a global sign to recover a plus sign in the field strength and covariant derivative instead of a minus sign.

and in the Wess-Zumino (WZ) gauge, the vector superfield (
V^{\dagger}=V
) takes the form

\displaystyle V

\displaystyle=\ \theta\sigma^{\mu}{\bar{\theta}}A_{\mu}(x)-\mathrm{i}\theta^{2}{\bar{\theta}}\bar{\lambda}(x)+\mathrm{i}{\bar{\theta}}^{2}\theta\lambda(x)-{\textstyle\frac{1}{2}}\theta^{2}{\bar{\theta}}^{2}\mathcal{D}(x)

(A.1)

\displaystyle=\ \theta\sigma^{\mu}{\bar{\theta}}A_{\mu}(y\hphantom{{}^{\dagger}})-\mathrm{i}\theta^{2}{\bar{\theta}}\bar{\lambda}(y\hphantom{{}^{\dagger}})+\mathrm{i}{\bar{\theta}}^{2}\theta\lambda(y\hphantom{{}^{\dagger}})-{\textstyle\frac{1}{2}}\theta^{2}{\bar{\theta}}^{2}[\mathcal{D}(y\hphantom{{}^{\dagger}})-\mathrm{i}\mathrm{D}^{\mu}A_{\mu}(y\hphantom{{}^{\dagger}})]

\displaystyle=\ \theta\sigma^{\mu}{\bar{\theta}}A_{\mu}(y^{\dagger})-\mathrm{i}\theta^{2}{\bar{\theta}}\bar{\lambda}(y^{\dagger})+\mathrm{i}{\bar{\theta}}^{2}\theta\lambda(y^{\dagger})-{\textstyle\frac{1}{2}}\theta^{2}{\bar{\theta}}^{2}[\mathcal{D}(y^{\dagger})+\mathrm{i}\mathrm{D}^{\mu}A_{\mu}(y^{\dagger})]

where

and
y^{\dagger}=x-\mathrm{i}\theta\sigma{\bar{\theta}}
parameterize (anti-)chiral superspace. The advantage of the WZ gauge is that

V^{2}=-{\textstyle\frac{1}{2}}\theta^{2}{\bar{\theta}}^{2}A_{\mu}A^{\mu}\ ,

(A.2)

whereas all higher powers vanish such that the power series

\mathrm{e}^{2V}=1+2V+2V^{2}

(A.3)

truncates at the second order. The non-abelian supersymmetric field strength
W_{\alpha}
and its conjugate are given by

\displaystyle W_{\alpha}\ =\ -{\textstyle\frac{1}{4}}\bar{\mathrm{D}}\bar{\mathrm{D}}\mathrm{e}^{-2V}\mathrm{D}_{\alpha}\mathrm{e}^{2V}\ =\ +2\mathrm{i}\lambda_{\alpha}(y\hphantom{{}^{\dagger}})-2\bigl[\delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}_{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\mathcal{D}(y\hphantom{{}^{\dagger}})-\mathrm{i}\sigma^{{{\mu\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}F_{\mu\nu}(y\hphantom{{}^{\dagger}})\bigr]\theta_{\beta}-2\theta^{2}\not{\mathrm{D}}_{\alpha{\dot{\alpha}}}\bar{\lambda}^{\dot{\alpha}}(y\hphantom{{}^{\dagger}})\ ,

(A.4)

\displaystyle\bar{W}^{\dot{\alpha}}\ =\ -{\textstyle\frac{1}{4}}\mathrm{D}\mathrm{D}\mathrm{e}^{-2V}\bar{\mathrm{D}}^{\dot{\alpha}}\mathrm{e}^{2V}\ =\ -2\mathrm{i}\bar{\lambda}^{\dot{\alpha}}(y^{\dagger})-2\bigl[\delta^{{{{\dot{\alpha}}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.77779pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.77779pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77779pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77779pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.77779pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.77779pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77779pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77779pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{{\dot{\beta}}}}}\mathcal{D}(y^{\dagger})+\mathrm{i}{\bar{\sigma}}^{{{\mu\nu}{{\dot{\alpha}}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.77779pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.77779pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77779pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77779pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.8716pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.373pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83784pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.77779pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.77779pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77779pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77779pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{{\dot{\beta}}}}}F_{\mu\nu}(y^{\dagger})\bigr]{\bar{\theta}}^{\dot{\beta}}+2{\bar{\theta}}^{2}\not{\bar{\mathrm{D}}}^{{\dot{\alpha}}\alpha}\lambda_{\alpha}(y^{\dagger})\ ,

in chiral superspace, with the superspace covariant derivatives
\mathrm{D}_{\alpha},\bar{\mathrm{D}}_{\dot{\alpha}}
. The chiral superfields (
\bar{\mathrm{D}}_{\dot{\alpha}}\Phi_{I}=0
,
\mathrm{D}_{\alpha}\Phi^{\dagger}_{I}=0
) have the simple expansions

\Phi_{I}\ =\ \phi_{I}(y)+\sqrt{2}\theta\psi_{I}(y)+\theta^{2}F_{I}(y)\ ,\qquad\Phi^{\dagger}_{I}\ =\ \phi^{\dagger}_{I}(y^{\dagger})+\sqrt{2}{\bar{\theta}}{\bar{\psi}}_{I}(y^{\dagger})+{\bar{\theta}}^{2}F^{\dagger}_{I}(y^{\dagger})\ ,

(A.5)

in chiral superspace
and the full superspace expansions

\displaystyle\Phi_{I}

\displaystyle=\ \phi_{I}(x)+\mathrm{i}\theta\sigma^{\mu}{\bar{\theta}}\partial_{\mu}\phi_{I}(x)+{\textstyle\frac{1}{4}}\theta^{2}{\bar{\theta}}^{2}\Box\phi_{I}(x)+\sqrt{2}\theta\psi_{I}(x)-{\textstyle\frac{\mathrm{i}}{\sqrt{2}}}\theta^{2}\partial_{\mu}\psi_{I}(x)\sigma^{\mu}{\bar{\theta}}+\theta^{2}F_{I}(x)\ ,

(A.6)

\displaystyle\Phi^{\dagger}_{I}

\displaystyle=\ \phi^{\dagger}_{I}(x)-\mathrm{i}\theta\sigma^{\mu}{\bar{\theta}}\partial_{\mu}\phi^{\dagger}_{I}(x)+{\textstyle\frac{1}{4}}\theta^{2}{\bar{\theta}}^{2}\Box\phi^{\dagger}_{I}(x)+\sqrt{2}{\bar{\theta}}{\bar{\psi}}_{I}(x)+{\textstyle\frac{\mathrm{i}}{\sqrt{2}}}{\bar{\theta}}^{2}\theta\sigma^{\mu}\partial_{\mu}{\bar{\psi}}_{I}(x)+{\bar{\theta}}^{2}F^{\dagger}_{I}(x)\ .

For the construction of the coupling flow operator we also need the penultimate components of the various contributions to (
2.8
). For the first part, we find

\displaystyle{\textstyle\frac{1}{4}}W^{\alpha}W_{\alpha}

\displaystyle=-\lambda^{2}+\bigl[-2\mathrm{i}\mathcal{D}\lambda-2F_{\mu\nu}\lambda\sigma^{\mu\nu}\bigr]\theta+\bigl[-2\mathrm{i}\lambda\sigma^{\mu}\mathrm{D}_{\mu}\bar{\lambda}-{\textstyle\frac{1}{2}}F^{\mu\nu}F_{\mu\nu}+\mathcal{D}^{2}+{\textstyle\frac{\mathrm{i}}{4}}F^{\mu\nu}F^{\rho\lambda}\epsilon_{\mu\nu\rho\lambda}\bigr]\theta^{2}\ ,

(A.7)

\displaystyle{\textstyle\frac{1}{4}}\bar{W}_{\dot{\alpha}}\bar{W}^{\dot{\alpha}}

\displaystyle=-\bar{\lambda}^{2}+\bigl[+2\mathrm{i}\mathcal{D}\bar{\lambda}-2F_{\mu\nu}\bar{\lambda}{\bar{\sigma}}^{\mu\nu}\bigr]{\bar{\theta}}+\bigl[+2\mathrm{i}\mathrm{D}_{\mu}\lambda\sigma^{\mu}\bar{\lambda}-{\textstyle\frac{1}{2}}F^{\mu\nu}F_{\mu\nu}+\mathcal{D}^{2}-{\textstyle\frac{\mathrm{i}}{4}}F^{\mu\nu}F^{\rho\lambda}\epsilon_{\mu\nu\rho\lambda}\bigr]{\bar{\theta}}^{2}\ .

Next, we evaluate

\epsilon_{IJK}\ \mathrm{tr}\ \Phi_{I}[\Phi_{J},\Phi_{K}]\ =\ \mathrm{i}\epsilon_{IJK}f^{abc}\Bigl[\phi^{a}_{I}\phi^{b}_{J}\phi^{c}_{K}+3\sqrt{2}\;\theta\ \psi^{a}_{I}\phi^{b}_{J}\phi^{c}_{K}+3\theta^{2}\bigl(F^{a}_{I}\phi^{b}_{J}\phi^{c}_{K}-\phi^{a}_{I}\psi^{b}_{J}\psi^{c}_{K}\bigr)\Bigr]\ ,

(A.8)

and the hermitian conjugate analogously. Lastly, we find

\displaystyle{\textstyle\frac{1}{n_{\mathrm{c}}}}\mathrm{tr}\ \mathrm{e}^{-2V}\Phi^{\dagger}_{I}\mathrm{e}^{2V}\Phi_{I}\ =

\displaystyle\Phi^{a\dagger}_{I}\Phi^{a}_{I}+{\textstyle\frac{2}{n_{\mathrm{c}}}}\mathrm{tr}\ [T^{a},T^{b}]T^{c}\ \Phi^{a\dagger}_{I}V^{b}\Phi^{c}_{I}+{\textstyle\frac{2}{n_{\mathrm{c}}}}\mathrm{tr}\ [T^{a},T^{b}][T^{c},T^{d}]\ \Phi^{a\dagger}_{I}V^{b}V^{c}\Phi^{d}_{I}

(A.9)

\displaystyle\ =\ ...\ +

\displaystyle\theta^{2}{\bar{\theta}}\bigl[-\mathrm{i}\sqrt{2}{\bar{\sigma}}^{\mu}\psi^{a}_{I}\mathrm{D}_{\mu}\phi^{a\dagger}_{I}+\sqrt{2}F^{a}_{I}{\bar{\psi}}^{a}_{I}+2f^{abc}\phi^{a\dagger}_{I}\bar{\lambda}^{b}\phi^{c}_{I}\bigr]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\bar{\theta}}^{2}\theta\bigl[-\mathrm{i}\sqrt{2}\sigma^{\mu}{\bar{\psi}}^{a}_{I}\mathrm{D}_{\mu}\phi^{a}_{I}+\sqrt{2}F^{a\dagger}_{I}\psi^{a}_{I}-2f^{abc}\phi^{a\dagger}_{I}\lambda^{b}\phi^{c}_{I}\bigr]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\theta^{2}{\bar{\theta}}^{2}\bigl[-\mathrm{D}_{\mu}\phi^{a\dagger}_{I}\mathrm{D}^{\mu}\phi^{a}_{I}+F^{a\dagger}_{I}F^{a}_{I}+\mathrm{i}\mathrm{D}_{\mu}{\bar{\psi}}_{I}^{a}{\bar{\sigma}}^{\mu}\psi_{I}

\displaystyle-f^{abc}\bigl(\mathrm{i}\phi_{I}^{a\dagger}\mathcal{D}^{b}\phi_{I}^{c}-\sqrt{2}\phi^{a\dagger}_{I}\lambda^{b}\psi_{I}^{c}+\sqrt{2}{\bar{\psi}}^{a}_{I}\bar{\lambda}^{b}\phi^{c}_{I}\bigr)\bigr]

\displaystyle+\ \textrm{total derivatives}\ ,

where we have left out terms of power 2 or less in
\theta
and the traces over the
\operatorname{SU}(n_{\mathrm{c}})
generators were evaluated with

[T^{a},T^{b}]\ =\ \mathrm{i}f^{abc}T^{c}\ ,\qquad\mathrm{tr}\ T^{a}T^{b}\ =\ n_{\mathrm{c}}\delta^{ab}\ .

(A.10)

From (
2.8
) and (
A.7
), (
A.8
), (
A.9
),
we deduce that in Weyl notation the Lagrangian can be written as

\displaystyle g^{2}\mathcal{L}\ =

\displaystyle-{\textstyle\frac{1}{4}}F^{a}_{\mu\nu}F^{a\mu\nu}-\mathrm{i}\lambda^{a}\sigma^{\mu}\mathrm{D}_{\mu}\bar{\lambda}^{a}+{\textstyle\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D}^{2}-{\textstyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}}\epsilon_{IJK}f^{abc}\bigl(F^{a}_{I}\phi_{J}^{b}\phi_{K}^{c}+F^{a\dagger}_{I}\phi_{J}^{b\dagger}\phi_{K}^{c\dagger}\bigr)

(A.11)

\displaystyle-\mathrm{D}_{\mu}\phi^{a\dagger}_{I}\mathrm{D}^{\mu}\phi^{a}_{I}-\mathrm{i}\psi_{I}^{a}\sigma^{\mu}\mathrm{D}_{\mu}{\bar{\psi}}_{I}+F^{a\dagger}_{I}F^{a}_{I}+{\textstyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}}\epsilon_{IJK}f^{abc}\bigl(\phi^{a}_{I}\psi^{b}_{J}\psi^{c}_{K}+\phi^{a\dagger}_{I}{\bar{\psi}}^{b}_{J}{\bar{\psi}}^{c}_{K}\bigr)

\displaystyle-\sqrt{2}f^{abc}\bigl(\psi_{I}^{a}\lambda^{b}\phi^{c\dagger}_{I}+{\bar{\psi}}^{a}_{I}\bar{\lambda}^{b}\phi^{c}_{I}\bigr)-\mathrm{i}f^{abc}\phi_{I}^{a\dagger}\mathcal{D}^{b}\phi_{I}^{c}\ ,

up to total derivatives. From the superspace expansions we read off the supersymmetry transformations

\displaystyle\delta\phi_{I}\ =\ \sqrt{2}\theta\psi_{I}\ ,\qquad\delta\psi_{I}\ =\ \mathrm{i}\sqrt{2}\sigma^{\mu}{\bar{\theta}}\mathrm{D}_{\mu}\phi_{I}+\sqrt{2}\theta F_{I}\ ,\qquad\delta F_{I}\ =\ \mathrm{i}\sqrt{2}{\bar{\theta}}{\bar{\sigma}}^{\mu}\mathrm{D}_{\mu}\psi_{I}-2\phi_{I}{\times}\bar{\lambda}{\bar{\theta}}\ ,

(A.12)

\displaystyle\delta A^{\mu}\ =\ -\mathrm{i}\bar{\lambda}{\bar{\sigma}}^{\mu}\theta+\mathrm{i}{\bar{\theta}}{\bar{\sigma}}^{\mu}\lambda\ ,\qquad\delta\lambda\ =\ \sigma^{\mu\nu}\theta F_{\mu\nu}+\mathrm{i}\theta\mathcal{D}\ ,\qquad\delta\mathcal{D}\ =\ -\theta\sigma^{\mu}\mathrm{D}_{\mu}\bar{\lambda}-\mathrm{D}_{\mu}\lambda\sigma^{\mu}{\bar{\theta}}\ .

For convenience, we translate the superfield formalism to a four-component Majorana basis using

\displaystyle\lambda^{(\mathrm{M})}\ =\ \begin{pmatrix}\lambda_{\alpha}\\
\bar{\lambda}^{\dot{\alpha}}\end{pmatrix}\ ,\qquad\bar{\lambda}^{(\mathrm{M})}\ =\ (\lambda^{\alpha},\ \bar{\lambda}_{\dot{\alpha}})\ ,\qquad\alpha\ =\ \begin{pmatrix}\theta_{\alpha}\\
{\bar{\theta}}^{\dot{\alpha}}\end{pmatrix}\ ,\qquad\bar{\alpha}\ =\ (\theta^{\alpha},\ {\bar{\theta}}_{\dot{\alpha}})\

(A.13)

\displaystyle\gamma_{\mu}\ =\ \begin{pmatrix}0&\sigma_{\mu}\\
{\bar{\sigma}}_{\mu}&0\end{pmatrix}\ ,\qquad\gamma_{5}\ =\ \gamma_{0}\gamma_{1}\gamma_{2}\gamma_{3}\ =\ \begin{pmatrix}-i&0\\
0&i\end{pmatrix}\ ,\qquad\mathrm{etc.}\ ,

so that

\displaystyle\bar{\lambda}^{(\mathrm{M})}\lambda^{(\mathrm{M})}\ =\ \lambda\lambda+\bar{\lambda}\bar{\lambda}\ ,\qquad\bar{\lambda}^{(\mathrm{M})}\mathrm{i}\gamma_{5}\lambda^{(\mathrm{M})}\ =\ \lambda\lambda-\bar{\lambda}\bar{\lambda}\ ,

(A.14)

\displaystyle\bar{\lambda}^{(\mathrm{M})}\gamma^{\mu}\lambda^{(\mathrm{M})}\ =\ \lambda\sigma^{\mu}\bar{\lambda}+\bar{\lambda}{\bar{\sigma}}^{\mu}\lambda\ =\ 2\lambda\sigma^{\mu}\bar{\lambda}\ ,\qquad\frac{1}{2}\bar{\lambda}^{(\mathrm{M})}\gamma^{\mu\nu}\alpha\ =\ \lambda\sigma^{\mu\nu}\theta+\bar{\lambda}{\bar{\sigma}}^{\mu\nu}{\bar{\theta}}\ ,\qquad\text{etc.}\ ,

where the l.h.s. are in the four-component Majorana basis and the r.h.s. are in the two-component Weyl basis. Additionally, we need the chiral projectors

\mathrm{P}^{\pm}\ =\ {\textstyle\frac{1}{2}}(1\pm\mathrm{i}\gamma_{5})\ ,\qquad\bar{\lambda}^{(\mathrm{M})}\mathrm{P}^{+}\lambda^{(\mathrm{M})}\ =\ \lambda\lambda\ ,\quad\bar{\lambda}^{(\mathrm{M})}\mathrm{P}^{-}\lambda^{(\mathrm{M})}\ =\ \bar{\lambda}\bar{\lambda}\ .

(A.15)

This leads to the Lagrangian in Majorana notation (
2.10
) (leaving the superscript
(M)
implicit from now on) and to the penultimate component

\displaystyle\mathring{\Delta}\ =

\displaystyle\bar{\alpha}\Bigl\{-\mathcal{D}\gamma_{5}\lambda-{\textstyle\frac{1}{2}}F_{\mu\nu}\gamma^{\mu\nu}\lambda+2\epsilon_{IJK}f^{abc}\bigl[\mathrm{P}^{+}\psi^{a}_{I}\phi^{b}_{J}\phi^{c}_{K}+\mathrm{P}^{-}\psi^{a}_{I}\phi^{b\dagger}_{J}\phi^{c\dagger}_{K}\bigr]+2\mathrm{i}f^{abc}\gamma_{5}\phi_{I}^{a\dagger}\lambda^{b}\phi^{c}_{I}

(A.16)

\displaystyle+\mathrm{i}\sqrt{2}\bigl[\gamma^{\mu}\mathrm{P}^{-}\psi^{a}_{I}\mathrm{D}_{\mu}\phi^{a}_{I}+\gamma^{\mu}\mathrm{P}^{+}\psi^{a}_{I}\mathrm{D}_{\mu}\phi^{a\dagger}_{I}\bigr]-\sqrt{2}\bigl[\mathrm{P}^{+}\psi^{a}_{I}F^{a\dagger}_{I}+\mathrm{P}^{-}\psi^{a}_{I}F^{a}_{I}\bigr]\Bigr\}\ .

For completeness, we note the following hermiticity properties for Majorana spinors
\chi,\xi
:

\displaystyle\bar{\chi}\xi\ =\ \bar{\xi}\chi\ ,\qquad\bar{\chi}\gamma^{\mu}\xi\ =\ -\bar{\xi}\gamma^{\mu}\chi\ ,\qquad\bar{\chi}\gamma_{5}\xi\ =\ \bar{\xi}\gamma_{5}\chi\ ,\qquad\bar{\chi}\gamma^{\mu}\gamma_{5}\xi\ =\ \bar{\xi}\gamma^{\mu}\gamma_{5}\chi\ ,

(A.17)

\displaystyle\bar{\chi}\gamma^{\mu\nu}\xi\ =\ -\bar{\xi}\gamma^{\mu\nu}\chi\ ,\qquad\bar{\chi}\gamma^{\mu\nu}\gamma_{5}\xi\ =\ -\bar{\xi}\gamma^{\mu\nu}\gamma_{5}\chi\ ,\qquad\bar{\chi}\gamma^{\rho\lambda}\gamma_{\mu}\xi\ =\ \bar{\xi}\gamma_{\mu}\gamma^{\rho\lambda}\chi\ .

Consistency checks.
In order to cross-check the expressions above (in the Majorana basis), we performed three consistency checks. Firstly, due to the superfield structure, the penultimate component
\mathring{\Delta}
has to generate the Lagrangian via its supervariation up to total derivatives

{\textstyle\frac{1}{4}}\delta\mathring{\Delta}\bigr|_{\bar{\alpha}\alpha}\ =\ g^{2}\mathcal{L}\ ,

(A.18)

with
\bar{\alpha}(\ldots)\alpha\bigr|_{\bar{\alpha}\alpha}=-{\textstyle\frac{1}{2}}\mathrm{tr}(\ldots)
. In practice, this requires the Fierz identity for Majorana spinors

4\xi\bar{\chi}=-(\bar{\chi}\xi)+\gamma_{\mu}(\bar{\chi}\gamma^{\mu}\xi)+{\textstyle\frac{1}{2}}\gamma_{\mu\nu}(\bar{\chi}\gamma^{\mu\nu}\xi)+\gamma_{5}\gamma_{\mu}(\bar{\chi}\gamma_{5}\gamma^{\mu}\xi)+\gamma_{5}(\bar{\chi}\gamma_{5}\xi)\ .

(A.19)

A second check is making sure that the Lagrangian transforms as a divergence, i.e.

\delta\mathcal{L}\ =\ \text{divergence}\ .

(A.20)

A third consistency check is verifying the generation of the susy-algebra

\{Q_{\alpha},\bar{Q}_{\beta}\}\ =\ 2{(\gamma^{\mu})}_{\alpha\beta}P_{\mu}\ =\ -2\mathrm{i}{(\gamma^{\mu})}_{\alpha\beta}\partial_{\mu}\ ,

(A.21)

up to a gauge transformation. With the supercharges
\delta(...)=\bar{\alpha}_{\alpha}Q_{\alpha}(...)
this can be evaluated by computing the commutator of two supervariations

[\delta^{(1)},\delta^{(2)}]=[\bar{\alpha}_{1\alpha}Q_{\alpha},\bar{Q}_{\beta}\alpha_{2\beta}]=\bar{\alpha}_{1\alpha}\{Q_{\alpha},\bar{Q}_{\beta}\}\alpha_{2\beta}\ ,

(A.22)

acting on each field. One finds that the susy-algebra reads

\{Q_{\alpha},\bar{Q}_{\beta}\}=-2\mathrm{i}{(\gamma^{\mu})}_{\alpha\beta}\partial_{\mu}-[\omega,\cdot]_{\alpha\beta}+G_{\alpha\beta}(A)\;,

(A.23)

where
\omega=2\mathrm{i}\not{A}
,
[\omega,\cdot]^{a}=f^{abc}\omega^{b}(\cdot)^{c}
and
G_{\alpha\beta}
is a gauge transformation
A_{\mu}\rightarrow A_{\mu}+\partial_{\mu}\omega
as required in the WZ gauge.

Stripping-off the susy parameter.
Lastly, we find it convenient to strip-off the susy parameter by setting
\delta\equiv\delta_{\alpha}\alpha_{\alpha}
and
\mathring{\Delta}\equiv\bar{\alpha}_{\alpha}\mathring{\Delta}_{\alpha}
. This yields the supervariations (
2.11
) and the penultimate component (
2.12
) (up to an overall normalization). We note that the fermionic supervariations have gained an extra minus sign, since

\bar{\chi}\delta\lambda\ =\ \bar{\chi}_{\beta}M_{\beta\alpha}\alpha_{\alpha}\ =\ \bar{\chi}_{\beta}\delta_{\alpha}\alpha_{\alpha}\lambda_{\beta}\ =\ -\bar{\chi}_{\beta}\delta_{\alpha}\lambda_{\beta}\alpha_{\alpha}\quad\Rightarrow\quad\delta_{\alpha}\lambda_{\beta}\ =\ -M_{\beta\alpha}\ ,

(A.24)

with some arbitrary spinor
\bar{\chi}
. In general, one has to be careful with sign-flips, because fermionic quantities anti-commute with each other.

Appendix B
Details on the canonical construction

In this appendix we give the detailed calculation for how to get from (
3.6
) to (
3.7
).
We need

\displaystyle\delta_{\alpha}X[\tilde{\mathscr{A}}]

\displaystyle\widetilde{R}_{\Sigma}\ =\ \tilde{\mathscr{A}}_{\Sigma}-{\textstyle\frac{1}{4}}\mathrm{tr}\Bigl\{(\mathscr{C}_{\Sigma})^{{{4}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{A}}}\bigl[{\textstyle\frac{1}{2}}\widetilde{S}^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{4}}}(2\partial\cdot\widetilde{A}-\widetilde{\not{A}}\times\widetilde{\not{A}})-\widetilde{S}^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}\widetilde{\Phi}^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{4}}}\times\widetilde{\not{A}}-{\textstyle\frac{1}{2}}\widetilde{S}^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\texts ,

(B.14)

where we flipped the order of the quantities in the trace for a more natural implicit color structure. To do so, we have used that
\widetilde{R}_{\Sigma}
is real and identities such as

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\bar{\psi}}\ =\ \psi^{\dagger}\;\gamma_{0}\ ,\qquad(\gamma_{0})^{2}\ =\ \mathbbm{1}_{4}\ ,\qquad(\widetilde{S}^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}})^{\dagger}\ =\ \gamma_{0}\;\widetilde{S}^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{A}}}\;\gamma_{0}\ ,\qquad(\mathscr{C}_{\Sigma})^{{{4}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{A}}}\ :=\ \gamma_{0}\ ((\widehat{\mathscr{C}}_{\Sigma})^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{4}}})^{\dagger}\ \gamma_{0}\ ,

(B.15)

\displaystyle\gamma_{\mu}^{\dagger}\ =\ \gamma_{0}\;\gamma_{\mu}\;\gamma_{0}\ ,\qquad\gamma_{5}^{\dagger}\ =\ \gamma_{0}\;\gamma_{5}\gamma_{0}\ =\ -\gamma_{5}\;\ ,\qquad\gamma_{0}\;\widetilde{\Phi}^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}\;\gamma_{0}\ =\ (\widetilde{\Phi}^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{A}}})^{\dagger}\ .

This leads to

(\mathscr{C}_{\Sigma})^{{{4}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{A}}}\ =\ \left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\delta^{{{4}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{A}}}\gamma_{\mu}&\text{for}\quad\Sigma\ =\ \mu\ =\ 0,1,2,3\\
(c^{i})^{{{4}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{A}}}&\text{for}\quad\Sigma\ =\ 3+i\ =\ 4,5,...,9\end{array}\right.\ .

(B.16)

Since
\widetilde{R}_{\Gamma}=\tilde{\mathscr{A}}_{\Gamma}+...

\mathrm{tr}\Bigl\{(\mathscr{C}_{\Sigma})^{{{4}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{A}}}\bigl[{\textstyle\frac{1}{2}}S^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{4}}}({\textstyle\frac{2}{g}}\partial{\cdot}A-\not{A}\times\not{A})-S^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}\Phi^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{4}}}\times\not{A}-{\textstyle\frac{1}{2}}S^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}\Phi^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox .

(B.17)

Now that the coupling is restored, with

(B.18)

and
\not{\mathscr{D}}{{}^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{C}}}}S^{{{C}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}\ =\ \delta^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}

(B.19)

with

and

(B.20)

In particular
S{{}^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{4}}}}|_{g=0}=0

contains no term of order

with

(B.21)

to rewrite the first term:

\displaystyle-{\textstyle\frac{1}{4}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\longleftarrow}}{{{\textstyle\frac{\delta}{\delta\mathscr{A}_{\Gamma}}}}}P^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94446pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94446pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.0625pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1875pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\Sigma}}}_{{{\Gamma}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.70836pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.70836pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.5389pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.52777pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\ \mathrm{tr}\bigl\{(\mathscr{C}_{\Sigma})^{{{4}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{A}}}\bigl[{\textstyle\frac{1}{2}}S^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{4}}}({\textstyle\frac{2}{g}}\partial{\cdot}A-\not{A}\times\not{A})\bigr]\bigr\}

(B.22)

\displaystyle\ =

\displaystyle-{\textstyle\frac{1}{8}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\longleftarrow}}{{{\textstyle\frac{\delta}{\delta\mathscr{A}_{\Gamma}}}}}P^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94446pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94446pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.0625pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1875pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\Sigma}}}_{{{\Gamma}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.70836pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.70836pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.5389pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.52777pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\ \mathrm{tr}\Bigl\{(\mathscr{C}_{\Sigma})^{{{4}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{A}}}\Bigl[\sum_{l=0}^{\infty}\bigl(-gS_{0}\not{\mathscr{A}}\bigr)^{l}{{}^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{4}}}}S_{0}({\textstyle\frac{2}{g}}\partial\cdot A-\not{A}\times\not{A})\Bigr]\Bigr\}

\displaystyle\ =

containing no term of order

which after defining

(B.24)

takes the simple form (

).

Appendix C
Infinitesimal free action condition

In this appendix we present a direct proof that the coupling flow operator in the Landau gauge (
3.30
) satisfies the three infinitesimal conditions (
1.10
) (
1.11
). The determinant matching condition follows from the other two conditions and the defining relation (
1.4
). The gauge condition (
1.11
) follows automatically from the form of the covariant projector (
3.13
). Thus, we have only left to show the infinitesimal free action condition

(\partial_{g}+R_{g})S_{g}^{\mathrm{b}}[\mathscr{A}]\ =\ 0\ .

(C.1)

The basic procedure of the proof is equivalent to the one in A.3 of
[
10
]
for
{\cal N}=\,1
SYM in
D=3,4,6,10
, but we have to take into account subtleties coming from the additional degrees of freedom in the
{\cal N}=\,4
case. We can write the bosonic action as

S_{g}^{\mathrm{b}}[\mathscr{A}]\ =\ \int\mathrm{d}^{4}x\;\bigl\{-{\textstyle\frac{1}{4}}\mathcal{F}^{\Sigma\Theta}\mathcal{F}_{\Sigma\Theta}\bigr\}\ ,

(C.2)

with

\mathcal{F}_{\Sigma\Theta}\ =\ \partial_{\Sigma}\mathscr{A}_{\Theta}-\partial_{\Theta}\mathscr{A}_{\Sigma}+g\mathscr{A}_{\Sigma}\times\mathscr{A}_{\Theta}\ ,\qquad\partial_{3+i}\ =\ 0\ ,\qquad\mathscr{A}_{\mu}\ =\ A_{\mu}\ ,\qquad\mathscr{A}_{3+i}\ =\ \varphi_{i}\ .

(C.3)

From these expressions, it is easy to find

\partial_{g}S_{g}^{\mathrm{b}}\ =\ -{\textstyle\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{F}^{\Sigma\Theta}\mathscr{A}_{\Sigma}{\times}\mathscr{A}_{\Theta}\quad\textrm{and}\quad{\textstyle\frac{\delta S_{g}^{\mathrm{b}}}{\mathscr{A}_{\Sigma}}}\ =\ \mathscr{D}_{\Theta}\mathcal{F}^{\Theta\Sigma}\ ,

(C.4)

with implicit integration. We first show the statement for the particular choice of the coupling flow operator (
3.24
) and afterwards generalize the result to the full Lie algebra
\mathfrak{su}(4)
. Concretely, we first prove that

(\partial_{g}+R_{g})S_{g}^{\mathrm{b}}[\mathscr{A}]\ =\ -{\textstyle\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{F}^{\Sigma\Theta}\mathscr{A}_{\Sigma}{\times}\mathscr{A}_{\Theta}+{\textstyle\frac{1}{8}}\mathscr{D}_{\Theta}\mathcal{F}^{\Theta\Sigma}\ \mathrm{tr}\bigl\{(\mathscr{C}_{\Sigma})^{{{4}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}S^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{C}}}\not{\mathscr{A}}{{}^{{{C}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{D}}}}\times\not{\mathscr{A}}^{*}{{}^{{{D}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{4}}}}\bigr\}

(C.5)

vanishes. To do so, we use the identities

\displaystyle{\textstyle\frac{1}{4}}\mathrm{tr}\bigl\{(\mathscr{C}_{\Sigma})^{{{4}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}(\bar{\mathscr{C}}_{\Theta})^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{C}}}(\mathscr{C}_{\Gamma})^{{{C}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{D}}}(\bar{\mathscr{C}}_{\Psi})^{{{D}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{4}}}\bigr\}\ =\ \eta_{\Sigma\Psi}\eta_{\Theta\Gamma}-\eta_{\Sigma\Gamma}\eta_{\Theta\Psi}+\eta_{\Sigma\Theta}\eta_{\Gamma\Psi}\ ,

(C.6)

\displaystyle(\mathscr{C}^{\Gamma})^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}\mathscr{D}_{\Gamma}\ S^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{C}}}\ =\ \not{\mathscr{D}}^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}\ S^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{C}}}\ =\ \delta^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{C}}}\ ,

(C.7)

\displaystyle(\mathscr{C}_{[\Sigma})^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}(\bar{\mathscr{C}}_{\Theta]})^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{C}}}(\mathscr{C}_{\Gamma})^{{{C}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{D}}}\ =\ -2(\mathscr{C}_{[\Sigma})^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{D}}}\ \eta_{\Theta]\Gamma}+(\mathscr{C}_{[\Sigma})^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}(\bar{\mathscr{C}}_{\Theta})^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{C}}}(\mathscr{C}_{\Gamma]})^{{{C}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{D}}}\ ,

(C.8)

that are similar to the ones used in
[
10
]
. Here we have introduced a ‘conjugate’
\bar{\mathscr{C}}
(in the Landau gauge), so that

\displaystyle\mathscr{C}_{\mu}\ =\ \mathbbm{1}_{4}\gamma_{\mu}\ ,\qquad\mathscr{C}_{3+i}\ =\ 2[(t^{i})^{*}\mathrm{P}^{+}-t^{i}\mathrm{P}^{-}]\ ,

\displaystyle\not{\mathscr{A}}{{}^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}}\ =\ \mathscr{A}^{\Gamma}(\mathscr{C}_{\Gamma})^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}\ =\ \not{A}+\Phi^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}

(C.9)

\displaystyle\bar{\mathscr{C}}_{\mu}\ =\ \mathbbm{1}_{4}\gamma_{\mu}\ ,\qquad\bar{\mathscr{C}}_{3+i}\ =\ 2[t^{i}\mathrm{P}^{+}-(t^{i})^{*}\mathrm{P}^{-}]\ ,

\displaystyle\not{\mathscr{A}}^{*}{{}^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}}\ =\ \mathscr{A}^{\Gamma}(\bar{\mathscr{C}}_{\Gamma})^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}\ =\ \not{A}+(\Phi^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}})^{\dagger}\ ,

with the Clebsch-Gordon coefficients
t^{i}_{AB}
as matrices in R-space.
It should be noted that (
C.6
) is only valid up to terms that vanish when contracted with fields in the adjoint representation of the gauge group due to the Jacobi identity in color space. We explicitly check (
C.6
) at the end of this appendix. The identity (
C.8
) follows from the analogous identity for the 10d gamma matrices

\Gamma_{\mu}\ =\ \mathbbm{1}_{8}\otimes\gamma_{\mu}\quad\textrm{and}\quad\Gamma_{3+i}\ =\ 2\begin{pmatrix}0&t^{i}\\
(t^{i})^{*}&0\end{pmatrix}\otimes(\mathrm{P}^{+}-\mathrm{P}^{-})\ ,

(C.10)

as well as the anti-commutation relation for the Clebsch-Gordon matrices

\{t^{i},(t^{j})^{*}\}\ =\ -{\textstyle\frac{1}{2}}\delta^{ij}\mathbbm{1}_{4}\ .

(C.11)

With these identities at hand, we can rewrite the first term in (
C.5
) as

\displaystyle-{\textstyle\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{F}^{\Sigma\Theta}\mathscr{A}_{\Sigma}{\times}\mathscr{A}_{\Theta}\ \stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{eq:id_fa_1}}}{{=}}

\displaystyle\textstyle{\displaystyle\frac{1}{16}}\mathcal{F}^{\Sigma\Theta}\mathrm{tr}\bigl\{(\mathscr{C}_{\Sigma})^{{{4}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}(\bar{\mathscr{C}}_{\Theta})^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{C}}}(\mathscr{C}_{\Gamma})^{{{C}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{D}}}(\bar{\mathscr{C}}_{\Psi})^{{{D}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{4}}}\bigr\}\mathscr{A}^{\Gamma}{\times}\mathscr{A}^{\Psi}

(C.12)

\displaystyle\ \stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{eq:id_fa_2}}}{{=}}

\displaystyle\textstyle{\displaystyle\frac{1}{16}}\mathcal{F}^{\Sigma\Theta}\mathrm{tr}\bigl\{(\mathscr{C}_{\Sigma})^{{{4}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}(\bar{\mathscr{C}}_{\Theta})^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{C}}}(\mathscr{C}_{\Gamma})^{{{C}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{D}}}\mathscr{D}^{\Gamma}\ \displaystyle\ \stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathrm{ibp}}}{{=}}

\displaystyle-{\textstyle\frac{1}{16}}\mathscr{D}^{\Gamma}\mathcal{F}^{\Sigma\Theta}\mathrm{tr}\bigl\{(\mathscr{C}_{\Sigma})^{{{4}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}(\bar{\mathscr{C}}_{\Theta})^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{C}}}(\mathscr{C}_{\Gamma})^{{{C}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{D}}}S^{{{D}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{E}}}\not{\mathscr{A}}{{}^{{{E}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.14795pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.14795pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83153pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7368pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscrip
