\displaystyle\ \stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{eq:id_fa_3}}}{{=}}

\displaystyle-{\textstyle\frac{1}{16}}\mathscr{D}^{\Gamma}\mathcal{F}^{\Sigma\Theta}\mathrm{tr}\bigl\{\bigl[-2(\mathscr{C}_{\Sigma})^{{{4}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{D}}}\ \eta_{\Theta\Gamma}+(\mathscr{C}_{[\Sigma})^{{{4}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}(\bar{\mathscr{C}}_{\Theta})^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{C}}}(\mathscr{C}_{\Gamma]})^{{{C}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{D}}}\bigr]S^{{{D}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{E}}}\not{\mathscr{A}}{{}^{{{E}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.14795pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.14795pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83153pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7368pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{F}}}}\times\not{\mathscr{A}}^{*}{{}^{{{F}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.14795pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.14795pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83153pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7368pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{4}}}}\bigr\}

\displaystyle\ =

\displaystyle-{\textstyle\frac{1}{8}}\mathscr{D}_{\Theta}\mathcal{F}^{\Theta\Sigma}\mathrm{tr}\bigl\{(\mathscr{C}_{\Sigma})^{{{4}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{D}}}S^{{{D}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{E}}}\not{\mathscr{A}}{{}^{{{E}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.14795pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.14795pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83153pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7368pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.89958pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{F}}}}\times\not{\mathscr{A}}^{*}{{}^{{{F}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.14795pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.14795pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.83153pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7368pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{4}}}}\bigr\}\ ,

where in the last step we used the Bianchi identity
\mathscr{D}^{[\Gamma}\mathcal{F}^{\Sigma\Theta]}=0
. This concludes the proof for the special case

L=\operatorname{diag}(-1,-1,-1,+3)
(and permutations thereof). To reach the full Lie algebra we make use of the fact that we can superimpose coupling flow operators with weight one, giving the Cartan subalgebra and that
S_{g}^{\mathrm{b}}[\mathscr{A}]
is invariant under R-symmetry transformations
\mathscr{A}\rightarrow\mathscr{A}^{\prime}
. From

0\ =\ (\partial_{g}+R_{g}[\mathscr{A}^{\prime}])S_{g}^{\mathrm{b}}[\mathscr{A}^{\prime}]\ =\ (\partial_{g}+R_{g}[\mathscr{A}^{\prime}])S_{g}^{\mathrm{b}}[\mathscr{A}]\ ,

(C.13)

we observe the transformed
R_{g}[\mathscr{A}^{\prime}]
also satisfies the infinitesimal free action condition, reaching all
L\ \in\ \mathfrak{su}(4)
.

Lastly, we prove (
C.6
) by explicitly checking the various possibilities of the open indices. The easiest case is the one with only gamma matrices

{\textstyle\frac{1}{4}}\mathrm{tr}\bigl\{(\mathscr{C}_{\mu})^{{{4}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}(\bar{\mathscr{C}}_{\nu})^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{C}}}(\mathscr{C}_{\rho})^{{{C}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{D}}}(\bar{\mathscr{C}}_{\sigma})^{{{D}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{4}}}\bigr\}\ =\ {\textstyle\frac{1}{4}}\mathrm{tr}\bigl\{\gamma_{\mu}\gamma_{\nu}\gamma_{\rho}\gamma_{\sigma}\bigr\}\ =\ \eta_{\mu\sigma}\eta_{\nu\rho}-\eta_{\mu\rho}\eta_{\nu\sigma}+\eta_{\mu\nu}\eta_{\rho\sigma}\ .

(C.14)

Next, we consider the case when there are three gamma matrices (modulo chiral projectors) in the trace, i.e. one of the four indices in the range 4 to 9 and the three others in the range 0 to 3. In that case, the trace vanishes since any trace over an odd number of gamma matrices vanishes and the r.h.s. of (
C.6
) also clearly vanishes because in each term there is a Kronecker delta that is zero. The next case is the one where two indices are in the range 0 to 3 and the other two indices are in the range 4 to 9. We have to distinguish three arrangements of indices

\displaystyle{\textstyle\frac{1}{4}}\mathrm{tr}\bigl\{(\mathscr{C}_{3{+}i})^{{{4}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}(\bar{\mathscr{C}}_{3{+}j})^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{C}}}(\mathscr{C}_{\mu})^{{{C}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{D}}}(\bar{\mathscr{C}}_{\nu})^{{{D}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{4}}}\bigr\}

\displaystyle=\ {\textstyle\frac{1}{4}}\mathrm{tr}\bigl\{(\mathscr{C}_{3{+}i})^{{{4}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}(\bar{\mathscr{C}}_{3{+}j})^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{4}}}\gamma_{\mu}\gamma_{\nu}\bigr\}

(C.15)

\displaystyle=\ (t^{i})_{4J}(t^{j})^{J4}\ \mathrm{tr}\bigl\{\mathrm{P}^{+}\gamma_{\mu}\gamma_{\nu}\bigr\}+(t^{i})^{4J}(t^{j})_{J4}\ \mathrm{tr}\bigl\{\mathrm{P}^{-}\gamma_{\mu}\gamma_{\nu}\bigr\}

\displaystyle=\ -2[(t^{i})_{4J}(t^{j})^{J4}\eta_{\mu\nu}\ +\ \mathrm{c.c.}]\ =\ \delta_{ij}\eta_{\mu\nu}\ ,

\displaystyle{\textstyle\frac{1}{4}}\mathrm{tr}\bigl\{(\mathscr{C}_{3{+}i})^{{{4}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}(\bar{\mathscr{C}}_{\mu})^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{C}}}(\mathscr{C}_{3{+}j})^{{{C}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{D}}}(\bar{\mathscr{C}}_{\nu})^{{{D}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{4}}}\bigr\}

\displaystyle=\ \mathrm{tr}\bigl\{[(t^{i})_{4J}\mathrm{P}^{+}-(t^{i})^{4J}\mathrm{P}^{-}]\gamma_{\mu}[(t^{j})_{J4}\mathrm{P}^{+}-(t^{j})^{J4}\mathrm{P}^{-}]\gamma_{\nu}\bigr\}

(C.16)

\displaystyle=\ -(t^{i})_{4J}(t^{j})^{J4}\mathrm{tr}\bigl\{\mathrm{P}^{+}\gamma_{\mu}\gamma_{\nu}\bigr\}-(t^{i})^{4J}(t^{j})_{J4}\mathrm{tr}\bigl\{\mathrm{P}^{-}\gamma_{\mu}\gamma_{\nu}\bigr\}

\displaystyle=\ 2(t^{i})_{4J}(t^{j})^{J4}\eta_{\mu\nu}\ +\ \mathrm{c.c.}\ =\ -\delta_{ij}\eta_{\mu\nu}\ ,

\displaystyle{\textstyle\frac{1}{4}}\mathrm{tr}\bigl\{(\mathscr{C}_{3{+}i})^{{{4}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}(\bar{\mathscr{C}}_{\mu})^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{C}}}(\mathscr{C}_{\nu})^{{{C}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{D}}}(\bar{\mathscr{C}}_{3{+}j})^{{{D}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{4}}}\bigr\}

\displaystyle=\ \mathrm{tr}\bigl\{[(t^{i})_{4J}\mathrm{P}^{+}-(t^{i})^{4J}\mathrm{P}^{-}]\gamma_{\mu}\gamma_{\nu}[(t^{j})^{J4}\mathrm{P}^{+}-(t^{j})_{J4}\mathrm{P}^{-}]\bigr\}

(C.17)

\displaystyle=\ (t^{i})_{4J}(t^{j})^{J4}\ \mathrm{tr}\bigl\{\mathrm{P}^{+}\gamma_{\mu}\gamma_{\nu}\bigr\}+(t^{i})^{4J}(t^{j})_{J4}\ \mathrm{tr}\bigl\{\mathrm{P}^{-}\gamma_{\mu}\gamma_{\nu}\bigr\}

\displaystyle=\ -2[(t^{i})_{4J}(t^{j})^{J4}\eta_{\mu\nu}\ +\ \mathrm{c.c.}]\ =\ \delta_{ij}\eta_{\mu\nu}\ ,

with all the other index configurations related to the three above by the cyclicity of the trace. The trace with only one gamma matrix vanishes due to the same reason as for three gamma matrices. We are left with the case

\displaystyle{\textstyle\frac{1}{4}}\mathrm{tr}\bigl\{(\mathscr{C}_{3{+}i})^{{{4}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}(\bar{\mathscr{C}}_{3{+}j})^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{C}}}(\mathscr{C}_{3{+}k})^{{{C}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{D}}}(\bar{\mathscr{C}}_{3{+}l})^{{{D}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{4}}}\bigr\}\ =

\displaystyle 4(t^{i}){{}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.07434pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.07434pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.98846pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.56319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{4I}}}}(t^{j}){{}^{{{IC}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.3142pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.3142pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.39107pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.56505pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}(t^{k}){{}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.43575pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.43575pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.36467pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.97476pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{CK}}}}(t^{l}){{}^{{{K4}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[11.19589pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.19589pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.96207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.9729pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\ \mathrm{tr}\ \mathrm{P}^{+}

(C.18)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 4(t^{i}){{}^{{{4I}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.07434pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.07434pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.98846pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.56319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}(t^{j}){{}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.3142pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.3142pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.39107pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.56505pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{IC}}}}(t^{k}){{}^{{{CK}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.43575pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.43575pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.36467pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.97476pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}(t^{l}){{}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[11.19589pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.19589pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.96207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.9729pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{K4}}}}\ \mathrm{tr}\ \mathrm{P}^{-}

\displaystyle\ =

\displaystyle 8\ (t^{i}){{}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.07434pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.07434pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.98846pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.56319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{4I}}}}(t^{j}){{}^{{{IC}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.3142pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.3142pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.39107pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.56505pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}(t^{k}){{}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[13.43575pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.43575pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.36467pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.97476pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{CK}}}}(t^{l}){{}^{{{K4}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[11.19589pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.19589pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.96207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.9729pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\ +\ \mathrm{c.c.}

\displaystyle\ =

\displaystyle 8\ [(t^{i}){{}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.07434pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.07434pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.98846pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.56319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{4I}}}}(t^{j}){{}^{{{I4}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.07434pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.07434pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.98846pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.56319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}(t^{k}){{}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[11.19589pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.19589pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.96207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.9729pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{4K}}}}(t^{l}){{}^{{{K4}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[11.19589pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.19589pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.96207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.9729pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}

\displaystyle+(t^{i}){{}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.07434pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.07434pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.98846pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.56319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{4I}}}}(t^{j}){{}^{{{IJ}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.17293pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.17293pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.72687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.0906pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}(t^{k}){{}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70047pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.50032pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{JK}}}}(t^{l}){{}^{{{K4}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[11.19589pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.19589pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.96207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.9729pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}]\ +\ \mathrm{c.c.}

The last expression can be evaluated with the explicit form of the Clebsch-Gordon coefficients (
2.27
)
and the identity

\epsilon_{IJM}\epsilon^{MKL}\ =\ \delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}}}_{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{L}}}_{{{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.33472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.38194pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}-\delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{L}}}_{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.33472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.38194pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}}}_{{{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\ .

(C.19)

We do not quite find the desired result, because we obtain additional terms when two of the indices
i,j,k,l
are in the range 1 to 3 and the other two are in the range 4 to 6. For example

{\textstyle\frac{1}{4}}\mathrm{tr}\bigl\{(\mathscr{C}_{3{+}I})^{{{4}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}(\bar{\mathscr{C}}_{6{+}J})^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{C}}}(\mathscr{C}_{3{+}K})^{{{C}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{D}}}(\bar{\mathscr{C}}_{6{+}L})^{{{D}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{4}}}\bigr\}\ =\ \delta_{IL}\delta_{JK}-\delta_{IK}\delta_{JL}-\delta_{IJ}\delta_{KL}\ ,

(C.20)

where only the second term on the r.h.s. would appear in the r.h.s. of (
C.6
).
However, we contract (
C.6
) with the
\varphi
’s in the adjoint representation of the gauge group. It turns out that the additional terms are proportional to

(\varphi_{I}{\times}\varphi_{J})\ (\varphi_{I+3}{\times}\varphi_{J+3})+(\varphi_{I}{\times}\varphi_{J+3})\ (\varphi_{J}{\times}\varphi_{I+3})+(\varphi_{I}{\times}\varphi_{I+3})\ (\varphi_{J+3}{\times}\varphi_{J})\ =\ 0\ ,

(C.21)

i.e. vanish by the Jacobi identity in color space.

Appendix D
Explicit computation of the Nicolai maps

We first collect a number of useful identities

\displaystyle\sum_{B}\Phi^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}\times(\Phi^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{A}}})^{\dagger}\ =\ \left\{\begin{array}[]{cl}-2\gamma_{5}\sum_{J}\varphi_{J}\times\varphi_{J+3}&\text{for}\quad A\ =\ 4\\
2\gamma_{5}\sum_{J}(-)^{\delta_{KJ}}\varphi_{J}\times\varphi_{J+3}&\text{for}\quad A\ =\ K\\
\end{array}\right.\ ,

(D.1)

\displaystyle\sum_{B}\Phi^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}S_{0}\Phi^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{A}}}\ =\ \left\{\begin{array}[]{cl}\sum_{j}\varphi_{j}S_{0}\varphi_{j}-\gamma_{5}\sum_{J}(\varphi_{J+3}S_{0}\varphi_{J}-\varphi_{J}S_{0}\varphi_{J+3})&\text{for}\quad A\ =\ 4\\
\sum_{j}\varphi_{j}S_{0}\varphi_{j}+\gamma_{5}\sum_{J}(-)^{\delta_{KJ}}(\varphi_{J+3}S_{0}\varphi_{J}-\varphi_{J}S_{0}\varphi_{J+3})&\text{for}\quad A\ =\ K\\
\end{array}\right.\ ,

\displaystyle\sum_{B}(c^{I})^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}S_{0}\Phi^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{A}}}\ =\ \left\{\begin{array}[]{cl}S_{0}\varphi_{I}+\gamma_{5}S_{0}\varphi_{I+3}&\text{for}\quad A\ =\ 4\\
S_{0}\varphi_{I}-\gamma_{5}S_{0}\varphi_{I+3}(-)^{\delta_{IK}}&\text{for}\quad A\ =\ K\\
\end{array}\right.\ ,

\displaystyle\sum_{B}(c^{I{+}3})^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}S_{0}\Phi^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{A}}}\ =\ \left\{\begin{array}[]{cl}-\gamma_{5}S_{0}\varphi_{I}+S_{0}\varphi_{I+3}&\text{for}\quad A\ =\ 4\\
\gamma_{5}S_{0}\varphi_{I}(-)^{\delta_{IK}}+S_{0}\varphi_{I+3}&\text{for}\quad A\ =\ K\\
\end{array}\right.\ ,

and

\displaystyle\sum_{B,C,D}\mathrm{tr}\bigl\{(c^{I})^{{{4}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}S_{0}\Phi^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{C}}}S_{0}\Phi^{{{C}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{D}}}\times(\Phi^{{{D}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{4}}})^{\dagger}\bigr\}

\displaystyle=\ \mathrm{tr}\Bigl\{-2S_{0}\sum_{j}\varphi_{j}S_{0}\varphi_{j}\varphi_{I}

(D.2)

\displaystyle-2S_{0}\sum_{J}

\displaystyle\Bigl[\varphi_{I+3}S_{0}\varphi_{J}\varphi_{J+3}-\varphi_{J}S_{0}\varphi_{I+3}\varphi_{J+3}+\varphi_{J+3}S_{0}\varphi_{I+3}\varphi_{J}\Bigr]\Bigr\}\ ,

\displaystyle\sum_{B,C,D}\mathrm{tr}\bigl\{(c^{I})^{{{K}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}S_{0}\Phi^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{C}}}S_{0}\Phi^{{{C}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{D}}}\times(\Phi^{{{D}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}}})^{\dagger}\bigr\}

\displaystyle=\ \mathrm{tr}\Bigl\{-2S_{0}\sum_{j}\varphi_{j}S_{0}\varphi_{j}\varphi_{I}

(D.3)

\displaystyle-2S_{0}\sum_{J}

\displaystyle\Bigl[\varphi_{I+3}S_{0}\varphi_{J}\varphi_{J+3}-\varphi_{J}S_{0}\varphi_{I+3}\varphi_{J+3}+\varphi_{J+3}S_{0}\varphi_{I+3}\varphi_{J}\Bigr](-)^{\delta_{IK}}

\displaystyle+4S_{0}

\displaystyle\Bigl[\varphi_{I+3}S_{0}\varphi_{K}\varphi_{K+3}-\varphi_{K}S_{0}\varphi_{I+3}\varphi_{K+3}+\varphi_{K+3}S_{0}\varphi_{I+3}\varphi_{K}\Bigr]\Bigr\}\ ,

where in (
D.2
) and (
D.3
) half of the terms dropped out due to
\mathrm{tr}\ \gamma^{\mu}\gamma^{\nu}\gamma_{5}=0
. The analogous formulae with
(c^{I{+}3})^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}
are given by (
D.2
) and (
D.3
) with all indices
I,J,K,...
replaced by
I{+}3,J{+}3,K{+}3,...
and vice versa so that e.g.

\displaystyle\sum_{B,C,D}\mathrm{tr}\bigl\{(c^{I{+}3})^{{{4}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}S_{0}\Phi^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{C}}}S_{0}\Phi^{{{C}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{D}}}\times(\Phi^{{{D}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{4}}})^{\dagger}\bigr\}

\displaystyle=\ \mathrm{tr}\Bigl\{-2S_{0}\sum_{j}\varphi_{j}S_{0}\varphi_{j}\varphi_{I+3}

(D.4)

\displaystyle-2S_{0}\sum_{J}

\displaystyle\Bigl[\varphi_{I}S_{0}\varphi_{J+3}\varphi_{J}-\varphi_{J+3}S_{0}\varphi_{I}\varphi_{J}+\varphi_{J}S_{0}\varphi_{I}\varphi_{J+3}\Bigr]\Bigr\}\ .

To evaluate the traces, we need

\displaystyle\mathrm{tr}\ \gamma_{5}\gamma^{\mu}\gamma^{\nu}\gamma^{\rho}\gamma^{\sigma}

\displaystyle=\ -4\epsilon^{\mu\nu\rho\sigma}\ ,

(D.5)

\displaystyle\mathrm{tr}\ \gamma^{\mu}\gamma^{\nu}\gamma^{\rho}\gamma^{\sigma}

\displaystyle=\ 4(\eta^{\mu\nu}\eta^{\rho\sigma}-\eta^{\mu\rho}\eta^{\nu\sigma}+\eta^{\mu\sigma}\eta^{\nu\rho})\ ,

\displaystyle\mathrm{tr}\ \gamma^{\mu}\gamma^{\nu}\gamma^{\rho}\gamma^{\sigma}\gamma^{\lambda}\gamma^{\eta}

\displaystyle=\ -\eta^{\mu\nu}\ \mathrm{tr}\ \gamma^{\rho}\gamma^{\sigma}\gamma^{\lambda}\gamma^{\eta}+\eta^{\mu\rho}\ \mathrm{tr}\ \gamma^{\nu}\gamma^{\sigma}\gamma^{\lambda}\gamma^{\eta}\mp...\ .

We now give some intermediate results for the various contributions to the Nicolai maps.

First order:

We have

\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\leftarrow}}{{R_{1}}}{{}^{(A)}}\ =\ {\textstyle\frac{1}{8}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\longleftarrow}}{{{\textstyle\frac{\delta}{\delta\mathscr{A}_{\Gamma}}}}}\Pi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94446pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94446pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.0625pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1875pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\Sigma}}}_{{{\Gamma}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.70836pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.70836pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.5389pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.52777pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\ \mathrm{tr}\Bigl\{(\mathscr{C}_{\Sigma})^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}S_{0}\not{\mathscr{A}}{{}^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{C}}}}\times\not{\mathscr{A}}^{*}{{}^{{{C}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{A}}}}\Bigl\}\ ,

(D.6)

where
\Pi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94446pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94446pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.0625pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1875pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\Sigma}}}_{{{\Gamma}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.70836pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.70836pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.5389pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.52777pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\ =\ \delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94446pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94446pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.0625pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1875pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\Sigma}}}_{{{\Gamma}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.70836pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.70836pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.5389pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.52777pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}-C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94446pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94446pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.0625pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1875pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\Sigma}}}_{{{\Gamma}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.70836pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.70836pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.5389pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.52777pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}
and
\partial_{3+i}\equiv 0
. Further expanding

\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\leftarrow}}{{R_{1}}}{{}^{(A)}}\ =\ {\textstyle\frac{1}{8}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\longleftarrow}}{{{\textstyle\frac{\delta}{\delta\mathscr{A}_{\Gamma}}}}}\Pi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94446pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94446pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.0625pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1875pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\Sigma}}}_{{{\Gamma}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.70836pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.70836pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.5389pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.52777pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\ \mathrm{tr}\Bigl\{(\mathscr{C}_{\Sigma})^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}S_{0}\Bigl[\not{A}\times\not{A}\delta^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{A}}}+2\Phi^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{A}}}\times\not{A}+\Phi^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{C}}}\times(\Phi^{{{C}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{A}}})^{\dagger}\Bigr]\Bigl\}\ ,

(D.7)

we find

R_{1}{{}^{(A)}}A_{\mu}\ =\ {\textstyle\frac{1}{8}}\Pi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\ \mathrm{tr}\Bigl\{\gamma_{\nu}S_{0}\not{A}\times\not{A}\Bigr\}\ =\ C^{\rho}A_{\mu}A_{\rho}\ ,\quad\forall\ A\ =\ 1,2,3,4\ ,

(D.8)

where we used
(\mathscr{C}_{\nu})^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}\ =\ 0
, as well as
\Phi^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}\times(\Phi^{{{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{A}}})^{\dagger}\ \sim\ \gamma_{5}
and
\mathrm{tr}\ {\gamma_{\nu}\gamma_{\rho}\gamma_{5}}\ =\ 0
. The remaining part is

R_{1}{{}^{(A)}}\varphi_{i}\ =\ {\textstyle\frac{1}{4}}\ \mathrm{tr}\Bigl\{(c^{i})^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}S_{0}\Phi^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{A}}}\times\not{A}\Bigr\}\ =\ C^{\rho}\varphi_{i}A_{\rho}\ ,\quad\forall\ A\ =\ 1,2,3,4\ ,

(D.9)

where we used that a trace over an odd number of gamma matrices vanishes.

Second order:

First, it is straightforward to obtain

(R_{1}{{}^{(A)}})^{2}A_{\mu}\ =\ 2C^{\rho}A_{[\mu}C^{\lambda}A_{\rho]}A_{\lambda}\ ,\quad\forall\ A\ =\ 1,2,3,4\ ,

(D.10)

(R_{1}{{}^{(A)}})^{2}\varphi_{i}\ =\ C_{\rho}\varphi_{i}C_{\lambda}A^{\rho}A^{\lambda}-C_{\rho}A^{\rho}C_{\lambda}\varphi_{i}A^{\lambda}\ \quad\forall\ A\ =\ 1,2,3,4\ .

(D.11)

With the perturbative expansion of the covariant projector

P^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94446pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94446pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.0625pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1875pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\Sigma}}}_{{{\Gamma}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.70836pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.70836pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.5389pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.52777pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\ =\ \delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94446pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94446pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.0625pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1875pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\Sigma}}}_{{{\Gamma}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.70836pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.70836pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.5389pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.52777pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}-\mathscr{D}_{\Gamma}G\partial^{\Sigma}\ =\ \Pi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94446pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94446pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.0625pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1875pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\Theta}}}_{{{\Gamma}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.13892pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.13892pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.81111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\bigl\{\delta^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.13892pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.13892pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.81111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\Sigma}}}_{{{\Theta}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.70836pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.70836pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.5389pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.52777pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}-g\mathscr{A}_{\Theta}\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}(-g\partial{\cdot}A\;\;C)^{k}C\partial^{\Sigma}\bigr\}\ ,

(D.12)

and the gaugino propagator (
B.19
), we further find

\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\leftarrow}}{{R_{2}}}{{}^{(A)}}\ =\ -{\textstyle\frac{1}{8}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\longleftarrow}}{{{\textstyle\frac{\delta}{\delta\mathscr{A}_{\Gamma}}}}}\Pi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94446pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94446pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.0625pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1875pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\Sigma}}}_{{{\Gamma}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.70836pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.70836pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.5389pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.52777pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\ \mathrm{tr}\Bigl\{(\mathscr{C}_{\Sigma}){{}^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}}S_{0}\not{\mathscr{A}}{{}^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{C}}}}S_{0}\not{\mathscr{A}}{{}^{{{C}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{D}}}}\times\not{\mathscr{A}}^{*}{{}^{{{D}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.75377pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.1929pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.99492pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{A}}}}\Bigl\}-{\textstyle\frac{1}{8}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\longleftarrow}}{{{\textstyle\frac{\delta}{\delta\mathscr{A}_{\Gamma}}}}}\Pi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94446pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94446pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.0625pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1875pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\Theta}}}_{{{\Gamma}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.13892pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.13892pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.81111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\mathscr{A}_{\Theta}S_{0}\partial^{\sigma}\mathrm{tr}\Bigl\{\gamma_{\sigma}S_{0}\not{\mathscr{A}}{{}^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}}\times\not{\mathscr{A}}^{*}{{}^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{A}}}}\Bigl\}\ .

(D.13)

It is easy to see that the second term gives no contribution using
\Phi{{}^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{B}}}}\times(\Phi{{}^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{A}}}})^{\dagger}\ \sim\ \gamma_{5}
and symmetry. Evaluating the traces, one finds

\displaystyle R_{2}{{}^{(4)}}A_{\mu}

\displaystyle=\ 3C^{\rho}A^{\lambda}C_{[\mu}A_{\lambda}A_{\rho]}+2C^{\rho}A_{[\mu}C^{\lambda}A_{\rho]}A_{\lambda}+2C^{\rho}\varphi_{i}C_{[\rho}A_{\mu]}\varphi_{i}

(D.14)

\displaystyle+\Pi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\epsilon_{\nu\lambda\rho\sigma}\sum_{J=1}^{3}[C^{\lambda}\varphi_{J}C^{\rho}\varphi_{J+3}A^{\sigma}-C^{\lambda}\varphi_{J+3}C^{\rho}\varphi_{J}A^{\sigma}+C^{\lambda}A^{\rho}C^{\sigma}\varphi_{J+3}\varphi_{J}]\ ,

\displaystyle R_{2}{{}^{(K)}}A_{\mu}

\displaystyle=\ 3C^{\rho}A^{\lambda}C_{[\mu}A_{\lambda}A_{\rho]}+2C^{\rho}A_{[\mu}C^{\lambda}A_{\rho]}A_{\lambda}+2C^{\rho}\varphi_{i}C_{[\rho}A_{\mu]}\varphi_{i}

(D.15)

\displaystyle-\Pi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}\epsilon_{\nu\lambda\rho\sigma}\sum_{J=1}^{3}(-)^{\delta_{KJ}}[C^{\lambda}\varphi_{J}C^{\rho}\varphi_{J+3}A^{\sigma}-C^{\lambda}\varphi_{J+3}C^{\rho}\varphi_{J}A^{\sigma}+C^{\lambda}A^{\rho}C^{\sigma}\varphi_{J+3}\varphi_{J}]\ ,

and

\displaystyle R_{2}{{}^{(4)}}\varphi_{I}\ =

\displaystyle C^{\rho}\varphi_{I}C^{\lambda}A_{\rho}A_{\lambda}-C_{\rho}A^{\rho}C_{\lambda}\varphi_{I}A^{\lambda}+2C^{[\rho}A^{\lambda]}C_{\rho}\varphi_{I}A_{\lambda}+C^{\rho}\varphi_{j}C_{\rho}\varphi_{I}\varphi_{j}

(D.16)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\textstyle{\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}}\epsilon_{\mu\nu\rho\lambda}[C^{\mu}\varphi_{I+3}C^{\nu}A^{\rho}A^{\lambda}+2C^{\mu}A^{\nu}C^{\rho}\varphi_{I+3}A^{\lambda}]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C_{\rho}\sum_{J=1}^{3}\bigl[\varphi_{I+3}C_{\rho}\varphi_{J+3}\varphi_{J}+\varphi_{J}C_{\rho}\varphi_{I+3}\varphi_{J+3}-\varphi_{J+3}\varphi_{I+3}\varphi_{J}\bigr]\ ,

\displaystyle R_{2}{{}^{(K)}}\varphi_{I}\ =

\displaystyle C^{\rho}\varphi_{I}C^{\lambda}A_{\rho}A_{\lambda}-C_{\rho}A^{\rho}C_{\lambda}\varphi_{I}A^{\lambda}+2C^{[\rho}A^{\lambda]}C_{\rho}\varphi_{I}A_{\lambda}+C^{\rho}\varphi_{j}C_{\rho}\varphi_{I}\varphi_{j}

(D.17)

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\textstyle{\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}}\epsilon_{\mu\nu\rho\lambda}(-)^{\delta_{IK}}[C^{\mu}\varphi_{I+3}C^{\nu}A^{\rho}A^{\lambda}+2C^{\mu}A^{\nu}C^{\rho}\varphi_{I+3}A^{\lambda}]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C^{\rho}(-)^{\delta_{IK}}\sum_{J=1}^{3}\bigl[\varphi_{I+3}C_{\rho}\varphi_{J+3}\varphi_{J}+\varphi_{J}C_{\rho}\varphi_{I+3}\varphi_{J+3}-\varphi_{J+3}C_{\rho}\varphi_{I+3}\varphi_{J}\bigr]

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle 2C^{\rho}\bigl[\varphi_{I+3}C_{\rho}\varphi_{K+3}\varphi_{K}+\varphi_{K}C_{\rho}\varphi_{I+3}\varphi_{K+3}-\varphi_{K+3}C_{\rho}\varphi_{I+3}\varphi_{K}\bigr]\ ,

\displaystyle R_{2}{{}^{(4)}}\varphi_{I+3}\ =

\displaystyle C^{\rho}\varphi_{I+3}C^{\lambda}A_{\rho}A_{\lambda}-C_{\rho}A^{\rho}C_{\lambda}\varphi_{I+3}A^{\lambda}+2C^{[\rho}A^{\lambda]}C_{\rho}\varphi_{I+3}A_{\lambda}+C^{\rho}\varphi_{j}C_{\rho}\varphi_{I+3}\varphi_{j}

(D.18)

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\textstyle{\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}}\epsilon_{\mu\nu\rho\lambda}[C^{\mu}\varphi_{I}C^{\nu}A^{\rho}A^{\lambda}+2C^{\mu}A^{\nu}C^{\rho}\varphi_{I}A^{\lambda}]

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle C_{\rho}\sum_{J=1}^{3}\bigl[\varphi_{I}C_{\rho}\varphi_{J+3}\varphi_{J}+\varphi_{J}C_{\rho}\varphi_{I}\varphi_{J+3}-\varphi_{J+3}\varphi_{I}\varphi_{J}\bigr]\ ,

\displaystyle R_{2}{{}^{(K)}}\varphi_{I+3}\ =

\displaystyle C^{\rho}\varphi_{I+3}C^{\lambda}A_{\rho}A_{\lambda}-C_{\rho}A^{\rho}C_{\lambda}\varphi_{I+3}A^{\lambda}+2C^{[\rho}A^{\lambda]}C_{\rho}\varphi_{I+3}A_{\lambda}+C^{\rho}\varphi_{j}C_{\rho}\varphi_{I+3}\varphi_{j}

(D.19)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\textstyle{\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}}\epsilon_{\mu\nu\rho\lambda}(-)^{\delta_{IK}}[C^{\mu}\varphi_{I}C^{\nu}A^{\rho}A^{\lambda}+2C^{\mu}A^{\nu}C^{\rho}\varphi_{I}A^{\lambda}]

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle C_{\rho}(-)^{\delta_{IK}}\sum_{J=1}^{3}\bigl[\varphi_{I}C_{\rho}\varphi_{J+3}\varphi_{J}+\varphi_{J}C_{\rho}\varphi_{I}\varphi_{J+3}-C^{\rho}\varphi_{J+3}\varphi_{I}\varphi_{J}\bigr]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 2\bigl[\varphi_{I}C_{\rho}\varphi_{K+3}\varphi_{K}+\varphi_{K}C_{\rho}\varphi_{I}\varphi_{K+3}-C^{\rho}\varphi_{K+3}\varphi_{I}\varphi_{K}\bigr]\ ,

for
K=1,2,3
.

Appendix E
Testing the conditions of the Nicolai maps

We explicitly check the three conditions for the four distinct Nicolai maps (
4.8
)-(
4.11
).

Check of the gauge condition:
The (Landau) gauge condition
\partial^{\mu}T_{g}A_{\mu}=\partial^{\mu}A_{\mu}+\mathcal{O}(g^{3})
follows from symmetry and
\partial^{\mu}\Pi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}_{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}=0
.

Check of the free-action condition:
The maps have to satisfy

S_{0}[A^{\prime}_{\mu},\varphi^{\prime}_{I},\varphi^{\prime}_{I+3}]\ =\ S^{\mathrm{b}}_{g}[A_{\mu},\varphi_{I},\varphi_{I+3}]\ ,

(E.1)

with the bosonic action

\displaystyle S^{\mathrm{b}}_{g}\ =\ \int\mathrm{d}^{4}x\;\bigl\{-{\textstyle\frac{1}{4}}F_{\mu\nu}F^{\mu\nu}-{\textstyle\frac{1}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{\mu}\varphi_{i}\mathrm{D}^{\mu}\varphi_{i}-{\textstyle\frac{g^{2}}{4}}(\varphi_{i}\times\varphi_{j})^{2}\bigr\}\ ,

(E.2)

\displaystyle F_{\mu\nu}\ =\ \partial_{\mu}A_{\nu}-\partial_{\nu}A_{\mu}+gA_{\mu}\times A_{\nu}\ ,

\displaystyle\mathrm{D}_{\mu}\ =\ \partial_{\mu}+gA_{\mu}\times\ \ ,

and
S^{\mathrm{b}}_{0}=S^{\mathrm{b}}_{g=0}
. Writing this condition out at second order gives

\displaystyle\int\mathrm{d}^{4}x\;\Bigl\{

\displaystyle\textstyle{\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}}A^{\prime}_{\mu}|_{\mathcal{O}(g)}\bigl(\Box\eta^{\mu\nu}-\partial^{\mu}\partial^{\nu}\bigr)A^{\prime}_{\nu}|_{\mathcal{O}(g)}+A_{\mu}\bigl(\Box\eta^{\mu\nu}-\partial^{\mu}\partial^{\nu}\bigr)A^{\prime}_{\nu}|_{\mathcal{O}(g^{2})}+{\textstyle\frac{1}{2}}\varphi^{\prime}_{i}|_{\mathcal{O}(g)}\Box\varphi^{\prime}_{i}|_{\mathcal{O}(g)}+\varphi_{i}\Box\varphi^{\prime}_{i}|_{\mathcal{O}(g^{2})}\Bigr\}

(E.3)

\displaystyle=\ \int\mathrm{d}^{4}x\;\Bigl\{-{\textstyle\frac{1}{4}}(A_{\mu}\times A_{\nu})^{2}-{\textstyle\frac{1}{2}}(A_{\mu}\times\varphi_{i})^{2}-{\textstyle\frac{1}{4}}(\varphi_{i}\times\varphi_{j})^{2}\Bigr\}\ ,

after integrating by parts on the left hand side.
The free-action condition (in the Landau gauge) was previously shown for the map in
\mathcal{N}=\,1

D=10
SYM, i.e. before dimensional reduction. It is clear that the condition remains valid for the reduced map (given by the black terms) and action. We therefore argue that we only have left to show that the blue terms have no effect on the l.h.s. of (
E.3
). Further using
\partial^{\mu}A^{\prime}_{\mu}=0
(at all orders), we can drop two terms on the l.h.s. of (
E.3
), so we are left with the condition

\int\mathrm{d}^{4}x\;\Bigl\{A_{\mu}\Box A^{\prime\mu}|_{\mathcal{O}(g^{2})}+\varphi_{I}\Box\varphi^{\prime}_{I}|_{\mathcal{O}(g^{2})}+\varphi_{I+3}\Box\varphi^{\prime}_{I+3}|_{\mathcal{O}(g^{2})}\Bigr\}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\text{blue terms}}}\ =\ 0\ .

(E.4)

Let us start with the map obtained from
A=4
.
We refer to the three contributions as
1
,
2
,
3
respectively. Dividing by an overall factor of
{\textstyle\frac{1}{2}}
and switching to a graphical notation, we find for the first part

\hbox to14.18pt{\vbox to14.18pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 7.09111pt\lower-7.09111pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {

{}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{{}}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}\Biggr\}\ .

(E.5)

The last diagram drops out since we can bring the
\partial^{\nu}
to the center through integration by parts and the overall anti-symmetry under
\mu\leftrightarrow\rho
(gaining a factor
-1/2
). Then, clearly
\partial^{\nu}C^{\lambda}=C^{\nu\lambda}
contracts to zero with the epsilon symbol as it is symmetric under
\nu\leftrightarrow\lambda
.
The second contribution is

\displaystyle\hbox to14.18pt{\vbox to14.18pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 7.09111pt\lower-7.09111pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {

}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-2.5pt}{-3.22221pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}

(E.7)

The first respective terms of
2
and
3
cancel each other by means of integration by parts and symmetry. The second term of
2
cancels the first term of
1
and the corresponding second term of
3
cancels the second term of
1
. We are left with the second line of (
E.6
) and the corresponding terms from (
E.7
):

\lxSVG@closescope \;
\lxSVG@closescope {{
{}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{{}}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}\ =:\ \sum_{I,J}Z_{IJ}\ \stackrel{{\scriptstyle!}}{{=}}\ 0\ .

We can integrate by parts in the first diagram, which gives two contributions, one of them canceling the fourth diagram. Similarly, the third and fifth diagram can be combined into one. In the second and last diagram we can make use of the anti-symmetry under
I\leftrightarrow J
to also integrate by parts in both diagrams (gaining a factor
-1/2
in each) and then combine them into one contribution. This way the six diagrams reduce to three:

\lxSVG@closescope \;
\lxSVG@closescope {{
{}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{{}}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}\ \stackrel{{\scriptstyle!}}{{=}}\ 0\ ,

(E.9)

where we used
\partial_{\rho}C^{\rho}=\Box C=\mathbbm{1}
. The condition (
E.9
) follows simply from the Jacobi identity (in color space). In conclusion, the blue terms of the
A=4
map indeed have no effect on the free action condition, at least to the second order.

For the
A=K
case, most of the calculation can be done in the same way as for
A=4
by simply carrying around the sign factors
(-)^{\delta_{KJ}}
etc. However, the third and last line in (
4.11
) require special attention. Referring to (
E.8
), the remaining condition for
A=K
can be written as

\sum_{I,J}(-)^{\delta_{KI}}Z_{IJ}-2\sum_{I}Z_{IK}\ =\ \sum_{J}\bigl(-Z_{KJ}-2Z_{JK}+\sum_{I\neq K}Z_{IJ}\bigr)\ \stackrel{{\scriptstyle!}}{{=}}\ 0\ .

(E.10)

In the following we will show that this condition is satisfied by demonstrating that

\sum_{J}Z_{JK}\ =\ -\sum_{J}Z_{KJ}\ ,\quad\text{ for any }\quad K\ =\ 1,2,3\ ,

(E.11)

and using our previous result
\sum_{I,J}Z_{IJ}=0
. In order to make clear that we are not summing over
K
, we will set
K=1
in the following, although the calculation works in the exact same way for
K=2,3
. We start with

\displaystyle\sum_{J}Z_{J1}\ =

\displaystyle\hbox to90.28pt{\vbox to54.77pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 37.41725pt\lower-26.25432pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}
\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin}

of which the first three cancel through the Jacobi identity and the fourth and fifth can be combined into one using the Jacobi identity once more. We find

{}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{{}}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}\ =\ -\sum_{J}Z_{1J}\ ,

which concludes our check of the free action condition for
A=K
.

Check of the determinant matching:
The map has to satisfy

\operatorname{log}\ \operatorname{det}\ \frac{\delta\mathscr{A}^{\prime}}{\delta\mathscr{A}}\ =\ \operatorname{log}\ \Delta_{\mathrm{MSS}}[A]\Delta_{\mathrm{FP}}[A]\ ,

(E.17)

which has been checked for the
\mathcal{N}=\,1

D=10
result (to fourth order). It is easy to convince oneself that the condition is preserved under dimensional reduction to
\mathcal{N}=\,4

D=4
. Hence, we will again only show that the blue terms have no effect on the l.h.s. of the condition. Using
\operatorname{log}\ \operatorname{det}=\mathrm{tr}\ \operatorname{log}
, we see that they first enter at the second order through the first term of

\operatorname{log}\ \operatorname{det}\ \frac{\delta\mathscr{A}^{\prime}}{\delta\mathscr{A}}\Bigr|_{\mathcal{O}(g^{2})}\ =\ \mathrm{tr}\ \frac{\delta\mathscr{A}^{\prime}}{\delta\mathscr{A}}\Bigr|_{\mathcal{O}(g^{2})}-{\textstyle\frac{1}{2}}\mathrm{tr}\ \frac{\delta\mathscr{A}^{\prime}}{\delta\mathscr{A}}\Bigr|_{\mathcal{O}(g)}\frac{\delta\mathscr{A}^{\prime}}{\delta\mathscr{A}}\Bigr|_{\mathcal{O}(g)}\ .

(E.18)

More specifically, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{tr}\ \frac{\delta\mathscr{A}^{\prime}}{\delta\mathscr{A}}

\displaystyle=\ \int\;\mathrm{d}^{4}x\;\mathrm{d}^{4}y\ \delta^{(4)}(x-y)\delta^{ab}\delta^{{{\Sigma}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.56947pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.56947pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.08334pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.91667pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.70836pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.70836pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.5389pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.52777pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\Delta}}}\frac{\delta\mathscr{A}^{\prime a}_{\Sigma}(x)}{\delta\mathscr{A}^{b}_{\Delta}(y)}

(E.19)

\displaystyle=\ \int\;\mathrm{d}^{4}x\;\mathrm{d}^{4}y\ \delta^{(4)}(x-y)\delta^{ab}\Bigl\{\frac{\delta A^{\prime a}_{\mu}(x)}{\delta A^{b}_{\nu}(y)}\delta^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}+\frac{\delta\varphi^{\prime a}_{I}(x)}{\delta\varphi_{J}^{b}(y)}\delta_{IJ}+\frac{\delta\varphi^{\prime a}_{I+3}(x)}{\delta\varphi_{J+3}^{b}(y)}\delta_{IJ}\Bigr\}\ .

We need to show that the contributions from the blue terms in (
E.19
) vanish. We again refer to the three contributions as
1
,
2
and
3
respectively and start with the case
A=4
. It is easy to see that

\hbox to14.18pt{\vbox to14.18pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 7.09111pt\lower-7.09111pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {
{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 9.54 0 C 9.54 5.27 5.27 9.54 0 9.54 C -5.27 9.54 -9.54 5.27 -9.54 0 C -9.54 -5.27 -5.27 -9.54 0 -9.54 C 5.27 -9.54 9.54 -5.27 9.54 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-2.5pt}{-3.22221pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}=0
,
since every term contains
\epsilon_{\mu\lambda\rho\sigma}\eta^{\mu\sigma}=0
. For
2
, we note that the first line of the blue terms in (
4.10
) contains no fields
\varphi_{I}
, i.e. drops out when varying w.r.t.
\varphi_{I}
. The second term in the last line gives no contribution since
f^{aac}=0
, whereas the remaining two contributions cancel each other. In an analogous fashion, one finds
\hbox to14.18pt{\vbox to14.18pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 7.09111pt\lower-7.09111pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {
{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 9.54 0 C 9.54 5.27 5.27 9.54 0 9.54 C -5.27 9.54 -9.54 5.27 -9.54 0 C -9.54 -5.27 -5.27 -9.54 0 -9.54 C 5.27 -9.54 9.54 -5.27 9.54 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-2.5pt}{-3.22221pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}=0
, so that in total, the blue terms leave the determinant matching condition (
E.17
) invariant. The
A=K
case works in almost the same way by carrying around the sign factors and additionally taking care of the last line in (
4.11
).
